### [141] [Learning Reachability-Based Safety Representations for Real-Time Robot Navigation in Unknown and Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16522)
*Bojan Derajić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出用神经网络离线学习Hamilton-Jacobi可达性值函数的"动力学修正项"，并将其嵌入优化控制器，实现未知动态环境下的实时安全机器人导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知、动态环境中，仅靠几何碰撞检测不足以保证安全：机器人即使离障碍物较远，也可能因自身速度、朝向或障碍物运动而无法避让。HJ可达性分析能通过值函数符号区分安全与不可避免碰撞的状态，但在线对每个新观测环境求解代价过高。

Method: 离线训练神经隐式表示来逼近可达性解。由于值函数不超过约束，其等于直接计算的约束（如符号距离）减去一个非负的动力学修正项，只学习该修正项以保证表示障碍物位于预测安全集之外。前四种方法分别将学习函数用作模型预测控制的终端约束（静态局部环境、预测障碍运动），以及控制屏障函数安全滤波器中的屏障候选（静态环境、通过相对坐标成对屏障处理数量变化的移动障碍）。第五种方法针对控制仿射动力学与多面体输入，利用HJ哈密顿量在顶点取最大值的性质，通过可并行的顶点输入树搜索生成训练标签，并用HJ变分不等式损失得到屏障候选。

Result: 地面与空中机器人的仿真及硬件实验表明，相比几何与人工设计的基线方法，所提方法在避碰方面表现更好，尤其在短预测时域和移动障碍物情形下。第五种方法将适用范围扩展到多达13个状态的系统，突破了前四种方法受网格离线求解器限制的低维模型约束。

Conclusion: 通过离线学习HJ可达性解并将其作为优化控制器的实时安全信息，可以在未知动态环境中实现优于几何与手工基线方法的安全导航，并通过基于树搜索与变分不等式的方法扩展到更高维系统。

Abstract: Safe robot navigation in unknown, dynamic environments requires more than geometric collision checking: a robot still far from an obstacle may be unable to avoid it because of its speed, orientation, or the obstacle's motion. Hamilton--Jacobi (HJ) reachability analysis captures this in a value function whose sign separates states that admit a safe continuation from those where collision is unavoidable, but computing it online for every newly observed environment is too expensive. This dissertation trains neural implicit representations offline on reachability solutions to give optimization-based controllers this information in real time. Since the value function never exceeds the constraint, it equals a directly computed constraint, such as a signed distance, minus a nonnegative dynamics correction. Learning only this correction keeps represented obstacles outside the predicted safe set despite approximation errors. The first two of five methods use the learned function as a terminal constraint in model predictive control, for static local environments and predicted obstacle motion. The next two use it as a barrier candidate in control barrier function safety filters, for static environments and, through pairwise barriers in relative coordinates, for a varying number of moving obstacles. Simulations and hardware experiments on ground and aerial robots show better collision avoidance than geometric and hand-designed baselines, especially for short horizons and moving obstacles. The fifth method replaces the grid-based offline solver, which limits the first four to low-dimensional models. For control-affine dynamics with polytopic inputs, the HJ Hamiltonian is maximized at a vertex, so a parallelizable tree search over vertex inputs can generate training labels. With a loss from the HJ variational inequality, these labels yield barrier candidates for systems with up to 13 states.

</details>


### [142] [Design and Analysis of a Nonlinear Parallel Mechanism Coupling for Dynamic Humanoid Legs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16604)
*Rohit Kumar,Heiner Peters,Felix Wiebe,Franek Stark,Janne Janzen,Dennis Mronga,Shubham Vyas,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将膝并联机构、踝并联机构与非线性膝-踝耦合相结合的串并联腿设计，驱动器全部靠近髋部，实现低远端惯性与关节间能力共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要轻量腿部机构以实现敏捷、动态且高能效的运动，通过近端驱动降低远端惯性是常见目标，但难以在紧凑结构下兼顾扭矩能力与高效力传递。

Method: 设计串并联腿：膝并联机构 + 踝并联机构 + 非线性膝-踝耦合，驱动器置于近端；建立耦合的非线性传动模型，并通过仿真与硬件实验进行验证。

Result: 耦合可在膝屈曲时被动辅助踝背屈以提升足部离地间隙，膝伸展时辅助跖屈以助力蹬地；共享传动使两关节互相借用驱动能力，降低膝峰值扭矩需求与踝快速运动时的峰值速度需求；6kg腿在足部等效反射质量为0.37kg。

Conclusion: 该设计证明了共享传动与近端驱动可在紧凑结构下兼顾扭矩与力传递效率，并实现极低的远端腿部惯性，适用于动态人形机器人。

Abstract: Humanoids increasingly demand lightweight leg mechanisms for agile, dynamic, and energy-efficient locomotion. Reducing distal inertia through proximal actuation has thus become a common design goal. Yet, compact transmissions that preserve torque capability and efficient force transmission remain hard to achieve. This paper presents a novel series-parallel leg design combining a knee parallel mechanism, an ankle parallel mechanism, and a nonlinear knee-ankle coupling where all actuators placed closer to the hip. As the knee flexes, the coupling passively assists ankle dorsiflexion for foot clearance and, as it extends it assists plantarflexion for push-off, redistributing ankle load through the knee transmission. We derive the coupling's nonlinear transmission models and validate them in simulation and on hardware. The design shows that the shared transmission lets both joints draw on each other's actuator capacity by easing the knee's peak torque demand under load and the ankle's peak speed demand during fast motion. %Together With the leg's proximal actuator placement, the leg achieves an effective reflected mass of 0.37kg at the foot for a 6kg leg, demonstrating very low distal leg inertia for dynamic humanoid robots.

</details>


### [143] [Language2Drive: Do Vision-Language-Action Models Really Follow the Instructions in Autonomous Driving?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16634)
*Zihan You,Zehua Fan,Wenqi Lyu,Aoqi Wang,Ni Wang,Yifei Yang,Linge Zhao,Huan Li,Sining Ang,Yuguang Yang,Jueyu Chen,Xiangyu Li,Qianshan Wei,Jieyuan Pei,Yuxin Wang,Zhiwen Chen,Jiaqi Li,Wenxuan Song,Yanyuan Qiao,Benjin Zhu,Haoang Li,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Language2Drive (L2D) 基准，用配对反事实单元 (PCU) 在固定场景下只改变指令，检验 VLA 驾驶模型是否真正按语言执行行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环基准只测路线完成、安全与规则合规，无法回答"语言是否真正决定了车辆执行哪一种可行行为"。轨迹可能既安全又成功，却违反甚至忽略乘客指令。

Method: 核心评测单元为配对反事实单元（PCU）：保持初始驾驶场景不变，仅更换指令，从而区分孤立指令成功与反事实配对成功，覆盖四类指令遵循范围。该基准同时实例化在闭环 CARLA 仿真、重建真实场景与开环驾驶基准中，并发布配套 CARLA-Language2Drive 仿真器与专用地图。

Result: 在多种评测环境下评估代表性驾驶 VLA，发现"驾驶能力强、语言控制弱"的显著错配：93.2% 的成功 rollout 在另一条同样可行的替代指令下不存在对应的成功结果。该失败主要表现为"单侧完成"——模型反复执行其偏好行为，当同一场景要求反事实替代行为时即失败。

Conclusion: 当前评测存在两大根本局限：一是孤立成功与驾驶分数会显著高估语言遵循能力；二是现有驾驶 VLA 在匹配的反事实测试下缺乏可靠的指令遵循能力。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models increasingly use natural-language instructions to condition autonomous driving behavior. Yet existing closed-loop benchmarks primarily measure route completion, safety, and rule compliance, leaving a fundamental question unresolved: does language actually determine which feasible behavior the vehicle executes? A trajectory may be safe and successful while still violating or even ignoring the passenger's instruction. To address this, we introduce \ltd, a benchmark designed to directly test this capability. Its key evaluation unit, the paired counterfactual unit (PCU), holds the initial driving scene fixed while changing only the instruction. This design enables us to distinguish isolated instruction success from counterfactual paired success across four instruction-following scopes. To test whether this distinction persists across evaluation regimes, we instantiate Language2Drive in complementary closed-loop CARLA simulation, reconstructed real-world scenes, and open-loop driving benchmarks, including our released CARLA-Language2Drive simulator with purpose-built maps. Evaluating representative driving VLAs across these settings reveals a striking mismatch between strong driving competence and weak language control: 93.2% of successful rollouts do not have a successful counterpart under the alternative feasible instruction. This failure is dominated by one-sided completion, where a model repeatedly executes one preferred behavior but fails when the same scene requires the counterfactual alternative. These results expose two fundamental limitations of current evaluation: isolated success and driving scores, which are widely used by conventional driving benchmarks, can substantially overstate language-following capability, while current driving VLAs themselves lack reliable instruction following under matched counterfactual tests.

</details>


### [144] [What Limits Cross-Task Generalization in World Action Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16641)
*Chen Zhao,Zipeng Ye,Yihan Lin,Yuchen Xie,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将世界动作模型（WAM）解耦为世界模型与逆动力学模型（IDM），提出相机空间IDM（AoE）作为统一"恢复接口"，诊断出生成未来在夹爪与交互几何上失效，并提出VITA损失，使生成未来的执行成功率从近零提升至47.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型在跨任务泛化上表现不佳，即使预测的未来画面看似合理的操作。作者希望找到这一差距的根源，并使其可被直接检验，从而明确监督信号应聚焦的区域。

Method: 1) 将WAM解耦为世界模型和IDM，固定IDM、替换未来图像来源，以检验差距来源；2) 提出Align Once, Evaluate Everywhere (AoE)——在大规模机器人数据上预训练的相机空间逆动力学模型，作为跨任务、跨环境的统一恢复接口；3) 基于诊断结果提出vision-to-action (VITA)损失：将生成未来通过冻结的AoE，惩罚恢复动作的差异，把生成引导向动作相关细节。

Result: 来自未见任务录制未来的恢复动作执行效果几乎与重放演示相当，而世界模型预测的未来在各基准上崩溃；失败集中于夹爪与交互几何，生成能力却消耗在任务无关背景上。在两个世界模型和三个操作基准上，VITA损失将生成未来的执行成功率从近零提升到47.0%，并迁移到WAM上带来原生控制的稳定增益。

Conclusion: 跨任务泛化的瓶颈不在生成能力本身，而在动作相关区域（夹爪与交互几何）的细节。通过冻结的通用IDM作为评估与监督接口，以VITA损失定向优化，可显著提升生成未来的可执行性，并推广至WAM的原生控制。

Abstract: Cross-task generalization remains difficult for world action models (WAMs), even when their predicted futures depict plausible manipulation. This gap can be attributed directly: we make this gap directly testable by decoupling a WAM into a world model and an inverse-dynamics model (IDM), then holding the IDM fixed while replacing the source of future images. We introduce Align Once, Evaluate Everywhere (AoE), a camera-space inverse-dynamics model pretrained on large-scale robot data, which provides this recovery interface for different future sources across tasks and environments. Through it, we find that actions recovered from recorded futures of unseen tasks execute almost as well as replayed demonstrations, whereas futures predicted by world models collapse across benchmarks. The failures are not generic: they concentrate on the gripper and interaction geometry while generation capacity is spent on task-irrelevant background. This diagnosis suggests that supervision should target exactly these regions. We therefore introduce the vision-to-action (VITA) loss, which passes generated futures through the frozen AoE and penalizes discrepancies in the recovered actions, redirecting generation toward action-relevant detail. Across two world models and three manipulation benchmarks, this simple change raises the execution success of generated futures from near zero to 47.0%. The same loss also transfers to WAMs and yields consistent gains in native control.

</details>


### [145] [FEM-Validated Real-Time Modelling of Soft Tactile Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16667)
*Silvia Terrile,Kaichen Shi,Taejun Kwon,Jiseok Lee,Nathan Lepora,Yeejin Lee,Saekwang Nam,Efi Psomopoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出结合有限真实数据、高保真FEM与图神经网络的数据高效框架，实现平面软触觉结构二维变形的实时精确预测，并验证了仿真到真实的有效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软触觉传感器建模困难：FEM计算成本高，实时模拟器对超弹性变形保真度不足；而脆弱传感器反复测试易损坏，难以采集大规模真实数据。因此需要一种兼顾精度与效率、且数据需求低的方法。

Method: 核心思想是解耦验证与训练：仅用82个相机追踪的接触构型来调参和验证ANSYS FEM模型，再由该FEM生成768个合成接触场景用于训练GNN。评估了正向预测（标记变形到接触定位）与逆向推断（接触构型到标记变形）两个任务。

Result: 验证后的FEM在超过90%的验证案例中误差小于0.5 mm；GNN代理模型平均绝对误差0.32 mm，推理速度为毫秒级（相比FEM的分钟级）。完全用合成FEM数据训练的模型在真实测量上仍保持亚毫米精度，显示良好的sim-to-real迁移。模型还被集成到实时可视化流程中。

Conclusion: 该框架在平面软触觉场景下实现了高精度、实时、数据高效的变形预测，支持虚拟原型与基于模拟器的设计探索，减少了对大量物理测试的依赖。

Abstract: Accurate modelling of soft tactile sensors remains difficult because finite element methods (FEM) are computa- tionally expensive, while real-time simulators often lack fidelity for hyperelastic deformation. Large physical datasets are also impractical to collect because repeated testing can damage fragile sensors. We present a data-efficient framework that combines limited real-world validation, high-fidelity FEM, and graph neural networks (GNNs) for accurate real-time prediction of two-dimensional tactile deformation in a planar soft tactile structure. The key idea is to decouple validation from training: 82 camera-tracked contact configurations are used only to tune and validate an ANSYS FEM model, which then generates 768 synthetic contact scenarios for GNN training. We evaluate both forward prediction from marker deformation to contact localisation and inverse inference from contact configuration to marker deformation. The validated FEM achieves sub-0.5 mm error in more than 90% of validation cases, while the learned GNN surrogate reaches a mean absolute error of 0.32 mm with millisecond-scale inference, compared with minute-scale FEM simulation. Models trained entirely on synthetic FEM data retain sub-millimetre accuracy on real measurements from the 2D tactile structure, demonstrating strong sim-to-real transfer within the tested planar setting. We further integrate the learned model into a real-time visualisation pipeline, enabling rapid deformation prediction for virtual prototyping and simulator-based design exploration without extensive physical testing.

</details>


### [146] [Belief Space Planning for Risk Signal Temporal Logic via Mixed-Integer Convex Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16696)
*Shaohang Han,Joris Verhagen,Kaier Liang,Jana Tumova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究高斯信念空间中的Risk STL (RiSTL) 规划问题，通过信息形式参数化信念并对信念动力学进行凸松弛，得到混合整数凸规划(MICP)，并证明其可靠、完备且保全局最优输入轨迹，最后用数值仿真验证方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人从含噪测量中估计状态，当测量所获信息依赖状态时，可通过信念空间规划主动降低不确定性。然而由此产生的问题通常非凸，基于梯度的方法既不完整也无法保证全局最优，因此需要一种能保证完备性和全局最优性的规划方法。

Method: 将Risk STL (RiSTL) 规划问题置于高斯信念空间中，采用信念的信息形式参数化，并对信念动力学进行凸松弛，从而构建混合整数凸规划(MICP)模型。

Result: 证明了所构造的MICP是可靠且完备的，且该松弛能保持全局最优的输入轨迹；并通过数值仿真验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究为信念空间中带时空约束的机器人任务规划提供了一种可保证完备性与全局最优性的凸优化框架，具有理论与仿真验证支持。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifies robot tasks with spatio-temporal requirements, and its robustness semantics allow planning by trajectory optimization. In practice, robots estimate their state from noisy measurements, and when the information gained from a measurement depends on the state, the robot can actively reduce its uncertainty by planning in the belief space. The resulting problem is, however, generally non-convex, for which gradient-based methods are neither complete nor globally optimal. In this paper, we consider Risk STL (RiSTL) planning in Gaussian belief space and show that an information-form parameterization of the belief, together with a convex relaxation of the belief dynamics, yields a mixed-integer convex program (MICP). We prove that the MICP is sound and complete, and that the relaxation preserves the globally optimal input trajectories. We validate the effectiveness of our method in numerical simulations.

</details>


### [147] [CopyQuad: Quadrupedal Skill Transfer from a Single Side-View Video Without a Depth Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16737)
*Mohamad H. Danesh,Raina Hu,Mengxue Xu,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CopyQuad 提出跳过深度估计或逐片段三维重建，用机器人自身站立宽度补全侧视相机缺失的横向轴，实现零样本四足模仿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频到机器人的四足模仿流程需插入学习式提升阶段（单目深度模型或逐片段三维重建）以恢复侧视相机看不到的一个轴，但该步骤复杂且未必必要。作者质疑：在矢状主导、肢体运动几乎位于像平面的侧视视频中，这条轴是否真的需要被估计。

Method: CopyQuad 将缺失轴固定为机器人自身站立宽度。使用冻结的二维关键点检测器与固定解析重定向，把单个未标定视频转为机器人参考轨迹，足端目标天然可达，无需逐片段轨迹优化。采用单一分阶段索引的奖励结构，每行为仅需共享扫描中的三个标量。

Result: 该奖励结构学到狗步、马步、马小跑和两个非周期手势，全部零样本部署到硬件。在免训练刚性测试中，视频深度模型把动物两髋距离变化为其均值的 40%–107%，单图度量模型为 22%–58%，而该距离本不可动。动作捕捉显示狗步真实横向爪位移峰峰值为 4.5 cm，平均偏差约 1.4 cm，低于策略本身遗留的 3.5 cm 横向足误差。修复到可行性的深度从未优于常数，未修复的估计则使机器人倒地。

Conclusion: 所丢弃的横向轴精度低于流水线本身分辨率，因此侧视矢状运动模仿中无需深度或三维重建；常数补全既简单又足够。

Abstract: Video-to-robot pipelines for quadrupedal imitation insert a learned lifting stage, either a monocular depth model or a per-clip 3D reconstruction, to recover the one axis a side-view camera cannot see. We present CopyQuad, which omits that stage and pins the missing axis to the robot's own stance width instead. The setting is side-view footage of sagittally dominated motion, where limb motion is almost entirely in the image plane. A frozen 2D keypoint detector and a fixed analytic retargeting then turn a single uncalibrated clip into a robot reference whose foot targets are reachable by construction, so no per-clip trajectory optimization is needed. One phase-indexed reward structure, whose only per-behavior freedom is three scalars from a shared sweep, learns a dog walk, a horse walk, a horse trot and two non-periodic gestures, all deployed zero-shot on hardware. We then measure what omitting the axis costs. In a training-free rigidity test, a video depth model varies the distance between the animal's hips by $40$ to $107\%$ of its own mean and a single-image metric model by $22$ to $58\%$, though that distance cannot move. Motion capture puts the true lateral paw travel of a dog walk at $4.5$ cm peak to peak, a mean deviation near $1.4$ cm against the $3.5$ cm of lateral foot error our policies already leave, so the discarded axis sits below the pipeline's resolution. Depth repaired to feasibility never beats the constant, and the unrepaired estimates put the robot on the ground.

</details>


### [148] [Human-Like Redundancy Resolution for Real-Time Bimanual Humanoid Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16855)
*Chao Tan,Ming Shin Cindy Sung,Shuxian Liang,Chaoqun Wan,Zhen Huang,Xian-Sheng Hua*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种类人冗余分辨率框架，将自适应肘部引导与奇异鲁棒腕部跟踪相结合，实现实时的双臂人形机器人遥操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 7自由度机械臂对同一腕部位姿存在多个肘部构型，仅跟踪腕部会导致肘部漂移或构型突变；固定拟人约束虽稳定肘部姿态，却会阻碍接近奇异构型时所需的冗余调整。

Method: 框架包含：肩-腕几何重定向生成机器人尺度的腕部目标；自适应肘偏置准则利用偏置方向引导姿态并按偏置幅值缩放引导，使冗余自由度随人类手臂伸直而逐步释放；分层速度求解器优先保证腕部跟踪，并将自适应肘部引导投影到腕任务零空间。

Result: 固定腕目标消融中，自适应引导使平均肘方向误差相比去除该引导降低54.4%；工作空间边界压力测试中，奇异鲁棒跟踪相比无阻尼伪逆将峰值原始关节速度需求降低97.9%；真实双臂任务与跨平台重定向验证了对不同机器人形态的适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架在保证腕部跟踪精度的同时稳定了肘部构型，并在奇异附近保持鲁棒，具备跨机器人形态的实用性。

Abstract: Real-time bimanual humanoid teleoperation requires accurate wrist tracking while keeping arm configurations predictable. Because a 7-DoF arm admits multiple elbow configurations for the same wrist pose, wrist-only tracking can cause elbow drift or abrupt configuration changes. Fixed anthropomorphic constraints can stabilize elbow posture, but they can also block the redundant adjustments needed near singular configurations. We propose a human-like redundancy-resolution framework that combines adaptive elbow guidance with singularity-robust wrist tracking. Shoulder-wrist geometric retargeting generates robot-scale wrist targets. An adaptive elbow-offset criterion uses the offset direction to guide posture and scales this guidance by the offset magnitude, so the redundant degree of freedom is progressively released as the human arm straightens. A hierarchical velocity solver prioritizes wrist tracking and projects the adaptive elbow guidance into the wrist-task null space. In a fixed-wrist-target ablation, adaptive guidance reduces mean elbow-direction error by 54.4% compared with removing this guidance. In a workspace-boundary stress test, singularity-robust tracking reduces peak raw joint-velocity demand by 97.9% compared with the undamped pseudoinverse. Real-world bimanual tasks and cross-platform retargeting show applicability across robot morphologies.

</details>


### [149] [UniLAP: A Unified Latent Action Policy for Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16969)
*Lei Yao,Qiao Feng,Mutian Tong,Qihao Yun,Lap-Pui Chau,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 UniLAP，一种统一连续隐动作策略，通过流匹配建模隐空间，无需推理时依赖未来帧，结合流自适应目标蒸馏 LAM 教师并提取无标签运动目标，在多个任务上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Latent Action Models 使用离散隐变量存在粒度瓶颈，难以表示细粒度动作；连续替代方案多作为世界建模代理，推理时需额外规划步骤才能生成可执行动作，且隐表示易受任务无关外观干扰，限制跨平台迁移能力。

Method: 提出 UniLAP 统一连续隐动作策略：通过流匹配建模隐空间，绕过对推理时未来帧的依赖；采用流自适应目标蒸馏 LAM 教师，继承其动作表示并保留多模态可行行为分布；提取无标签运动目标，将相关运动学线索从虚假视觉动态中分离，实现隐表示与任务无关外观解耦，建立具身无关的隐流形以便适配新平台。

Result: 在多源视频上以 1.1B 紧凑参数规模预训练，UniLAP 在手部姿态预测、单臂与类人操作以及真实世界部署中，均优于最先进的隐动作基线与 Vision-Language-Action 基线。

Conclusion: UniLAP 通过连续流匹配隐动作建模与运动目标解耦，解决了表示粒度与推理规划瓶颈，实现了具身无关的高效迁移和优越性能。

Abstract: Latent Action Models (LAMs) can capture manipulation priors from action-free videos, but discrete latents usually impose a granularity bottleneck, limiting their ability to represent fine-grained action. Recent continuous alternatives mitigate this, but they are typically used as proxies for world modeling and require a further planning step to produce executable actions. We thus introduce UniLAP, a unified continuous latent-action policy that models the latent space via flow matching and bypasses reliance on future frames during inference. By distilling a LAM teacher with a flow-adaptive objective, UniLAP inherits the teacher's action representation while retaining the multimodal distribution of feasible behaviors. To decouple latent representations from task-irrelevant appearance, we extract a label-free motion target to isolate relevant kinematic cues from spurious visual dynamics. With the established embodiment-agnostic latent manifold, UniLAP can be easily adapted to novel platforms. Pretrained on multi-source videos at a compact 1.1B parameter scale, UniLAP outperforms state-of-the-art latent-action and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) baselines across hand pose forecasting, single-arm and humanoid manipulation, and real-world deployment.

</details>


### [150] [Joint-Feasibility Margin Feedback for Bounded-Input Second-Order Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16978)
*Siyuan Yang,Shaoming He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究二阶控制屏障函数（HOCBF）在有限加速度下多个约束会耗尽共同控制权限、导致松可行状态下的硬QP不可行问题，提出一种带符号的局部联合可行性裕度及其预测式反馈控制器以避免不可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个二阶CBF约束即便当前状态仍然松可行，也可能因共享控制权限被耗尽而在有界加速度下导致硬HOCBF二次规划不可行。论文动机是提前识别并规避这种联合不可行性，而不是等到QP失效后再处理。

Method: 定义带符号的局部联合可行性裕度，其符号恰好判定当前屏障半空间是否与容许输入集相交。对单位尺度距离约束，该裕度具有加速度量纲，并给出支撑函数对偶形式以识别加权约束冲突。控制器对九个任务导向候选进行一步超前预测，选择能达期望裕度的最小偏离候选，或选择预测裕度最大的候选，然后求解同一个硬HOCBF二次规划。

Result: 受控单步案例显示：在硬QP指令预测状态下，两个约束各自仍可行但联合不可行，而裕度反馈控制器仍保持正的下一步裕度。在20组配对初始条件中，裕度反馈控制器在低压任务中保持硬QP指令，在关键任务中以正裕度提前行动，从而在硬QP问题变为不可行之前规避失效；其轨迹在该时刻保持可行并完成任务。源码已公开。

Conclusion: 基于带符号联合可行性裕度的一步超前预测与候选选择，可在求解相同硬HOCBF二次规划的前提下预防联合不可行，在保持任务完成的同时提升可行性与安全性。

Abstract: Multiple second-order control barrier function constraints can exhaust their common control authority under bounded acceleration, even when the current state remains feasible. We define a signed local joint-feasibility margin whose sign exactly identifies whether the current barrier half-spaces intersect the admissible input set. For unit-scaled distance constraints, it has acceleration units and a support-function dual that identifies weighted constraint conflicts. We predict the margin one sample ahead for nine task-directed candidates, selecting either the least-deviating candidate that attains a desired margin or the candidate with the largest predicted margin before solving the same hard HOCBF quadratic program. A controlled one-step case shows that, at the state predicted under the hard-QP command, two constraints remain individually feasible but become jointly infeasible, whereas the margin-feedback controller retains a positive next margin. Separately, across 20 paired initial conditions, the margin-feedback controller retains the hard-QP command in a low-pressure task and acts at positive margin in a critical task before the hard-QP problem becomes infeasible; its trajectories remain feasible at that time and complete the task. Source code is publicly available. https://github.com/xirhxq/cbf/tree/robio2026-preventive-regulation

</details>


### [151] [MOSAIC: Mode- and Support-Aware Inference-Time Control of Robot Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17001)
*Sabrina Bodmer,Rahel Rickenbach,René Zurbrügg,Melanie N. Zeilinger,Colin Jones*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MOSAIC，一种模式与支撑感知的无梯度推理时控制框架，用于机器人扩散模型的适应与组合，无需重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式策略（如扩散模型）提供了表达力强的数据驱动先验，但执行时面临的情境常与训练数据不匹配，存在策略未学过的约束与目标。现有推理时引导方法依赖梯度（可能将样本推离学习先验），或使用低效、模式崩塌或平均成分布外预测的采样过程。

Method: MOSAIC将初始噪声与所有随机反向步扰动视为预训练去噪过程的联合输入，用完全去噪输出计算重要性权重来调整该输入的分布；以自适应惩罚平衡物理可行性与学习支撑的一致性，并通过模式级重要性加权更新保留模式、避免破坏性平均。同一公式可通过共享耦合代价联合引导多个独立训练的扩散模型，实现协同行为而无需联合模型。

Result: 在导航、灵巧抓取、未知几何运动规划以及使用独立训练机器人先验的协作箱子操作上进行评估，MOSAIC均能在新部署约束下适应并组合冻结的扩散先验。

Conclusion: MOSAIC提供了一种无梯度的推理时控制框架，可在不重训练的情况下适应与组合冻结的扩散先验，兼顾模式保持与支撑一致性。

Abstract: Generative policies such as diffusion models provide expressive data-driven priors for robot behavior, yet the situations a robot faces at execution time rarely match its training data and impose constraints and objectives the policy never learned. Existing inference-time guidance methods either rely on gradients that can push samples away from the learned prior, or on sampling procedures that can be inefficient, collapse modes, or average them into out-of-distribution predictions.
  We introduce MOSAIC, a mode- and support-aware framework for gradient-free inference-time control of robotic diffusion models. MOSAIC treats the initial noise and all stochastic reverse-step perturbations as a joint input to the pretrained denoising process and adapts a distribution over this input using importance weights computed from fully denoised outputs. Physical feasibility and consistency with the learned support are balanced using adaptive penalties, while mode-wise importance-weighted updates preserve modes and avoid destructive averaging.
  The same formulation can jointly steer multiple independently trained diffusion models through shared coupling costs, enabling coordinated behavior without requiring a joint model. We evaluate MOSAIC on navigation, dexterous grasping, motion planning with unseen geometry, and collaborative box manipulation with independently trained robot priors. Across these settings, MOSAIC adapts and composes frozen diffusion priors under new deployment constraints without retraining.

</details>


### [152] [Task-Space Motion-Force Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Precise and Compliant Control under Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17069)
*Quang-Nam Nguyen,Ludovic Righetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一的NMPC框架，直接在关节空间规划与控制，兼顾任务空间的运动与力需求，可跨平台、跨任务泛化，并实现力反馈闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NMPC控制律由OCP隐式决定，OCP设计需针对特定平台与任务，依赖大量专家知识和调参；且基于优化的方法多将交互视为刚性接触，扭矩解对机器人与环境动力学建模误差敏感。因此需要一种统一、可泛化且对未建模交互鲁棒的NMPC框架。

Method: 提出统一NMPC框架，直接在机器人关节上规划与控制，并满足任务空间的运动与力要求；研究该控制器固有的任务空间阻抗特性，分析其在未建模交互下的部分收缩性（微分稳定性）与微分无源性，从而在高层规划层闭合力反馈回路。

Result: 在物理硬件上验证：在冗余机械臂上实现混合运动-力控制，在四足机器人上实现柔顺运动，同时保留硬约束满足、最优冗余利用及突发交互下的实时规划等NMPC核心优势，并实现精确的运动-力跟踪。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了跨平台、跨任务的统一NMPC，解决了力反馈NMPC的长期难题，在保持NMPC核心优势的同时提升了对外部交互的鲁棒性与力跟踪性能。

Abstract: In Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC), the control law is implicitly determined by the solution of an Optimal Control Problem (OCP). Consequently, OCP design is often tailored to specific platforms and tasks, demanding extensive expert knowledge and tuning effort. To address this, we propose a unified NMPC framework that directly plans and controls the robot joints subject to task-space motion and force requirements and is capable of seamlessly generalizing across multiple robotic platforms and diverse tasks. Furthermore, optimization-based methods often treat interactions as rigid contacts, making the torque solution sensitive to the modeling errors of the robot dynamics and the environment. We study the intrinsic task-space impedance of our proposed controller, its partial contraction for differential stability and differential passivity under unmodeled interactions. As a result, our framework enables closing the force-feedback loop in the high-level planning layer to achieve precise motion-force tracking performance, addressing the long-standing challenge of force-feedback NMPC. Finally, we validate our framework on physical hardware, demonstrating hybrid motion-force control on a redundant manipulator and compliant locomotion on a quadruped, while preserving core NMPC advantages such as hard constraint satisfaction, optimal redundancy exploitation, and real-time planning under unforeseen interactions.

</details>


### [153] [Declarative Reactive Robot Behaviors from Imperative Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17083)
*Vincent Berenz,Tasha Kim,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文探究LLM编码智能体能否生成用于机器人行为的声明式与反应式程序，使用Playful反应式脚本语言，通过专家编写的可复用编程指南引导智能体，从欠指定的自然语言描述出发，联合设计命令式组件与声明式行为逻辑，支持对话式增量细化和一次性生成完整行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM主要基于命令式编程语言训练，现有LLM机器人编程工作集中于以计划表达的目标导向任务。但许多机器人应用需要的不是任务，而是无终止条件的开放式、持续性感觉-运动参与模式（如迎宾、巡逻）等"行为"，这类行为更适合声明式和反应式编程，而LLM几乎没有相关训练。论文旨在探究能否弥合这一差距。

Method: 使用Playful这一反应式脚本语言，构建LLM编码智能体。在给定合适上下文和一组可复用编程指南的条件下，让智能体从欠指定的自然语言描述生成正确声明式、反应式程序。指南由专家一次性开发，编码了将智能体从命令式默认习惯转向正确反应式模式的设计原则，并可跨机器人和行为泛化。智能体仅从通用机器人操作系统API出发，联合设计命令式组件（用于感知、行动和评估的Python模块）与声明式组件（将二者组合为行为逻辑的反应式脚本）。

Result: LLM编码智能体能够为机器人行为生成正确的声明式与反应式程序。专家编写的指南可跨机器人与行为泛化。反应式编程的声明式特性同时支持通过对话轮次进行增量细化和一次性生成完整行为。

Conclusion: 通过合适的上下文和可复用编程指南，可弥合LLM在声明式与反应式编程上的训练缺口，使LLM智能体能够生成机器人行为程序，且反应式编程的声明式特性为编程交互提供了灵活支持。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are predominantly trained on imperative programming languages, and existing work on LLM-based robot programming reflects this: it focuses on goal-directed tasks expressed as plans. However, many robotic applications require not tasks but behaviors--open-ended, continuous patterns of sensory-motor engagement with no termination condition (e.g., greeting visitors, patrolling an area). Such behaviors are better served by declarative and reactive programming, on which LLMs have virtually no training. We investigate whether this gap can be bridged. Using Playful, a reactive scripting language, we show that an LLM-based coding agent can generate correct declarative and reactive programs for robotic behaviors. These programs are produced from under-specified natural language descriptions, given suitable context and a set of reusable programming guidelines. The guidelines encode design principles that redirect the agent from imperative defaults toward proper reactive patterns. Developed once by an expert, they generalize across robots and behaviors. Starting from only the generic robot operating system API, the agent jointly designs both the imperative component (Python modules for sensing, acting, and evaluating) and the declarative component (the reactive script that composes them into behavior logic). The declarative nature of reactive programming supports both incremental refinement through conversational turns and one-shot generation of complete behaviors.

</details>


### [154] [Interactive Navigation Beyond Reachability: Benchmark and Agentic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17089)
*Delun Li,Xueyao Wei,Rui Chen,Yifei Wang,Junzhi Yu,Wenjie Song,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出“超越可达性的交互式导航”任务，统一建模通道与容器引起的地图连通性和目标可见性变化及其顺序依赖，并发布了包含3000个episode的MultINav-Bench基准及三阶段智能体框架MultINav-Agent。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航任务普遍假设episode期间可通行性与物体状态保持不变，但实际到达目标常需要先开门进入房间、再打开容器才能显露隐藏目标，因此需要将导航与交互统一建模。

Method: 构建MultINav-Bench：基于MolmoSpaces的3000个episode ObjectGoal基准，覆盖通道、容器与混合任务，并带交互必要性标注；数据生成流程可自动构建多类任务；评估协议分别衡量导航成功、交互有效性和成本。提出MultINav-Agent：基于持久的、状态条件化的交互图，采用三阶段框架——语义交互感知进行实体与交互状态定位；视觉语言模型依据连通性与目标可观测性的预期变化选择任务相关动作；交互后验证更新图以指导后续导航、探索或交互。

Result: 在MultINav-Bench上，MultINav-Agent在通道、容器和混合任务中均持续优于适配后的基线方法。

Conclusion: 将导航与交互统一为“超越可达性”的交互式导航问题，并通过专用基准与基于交互图的智能体框架验证了其必要性与有效性。

Abstract: Existing embodied navigation tasks commonly assume that traversability and object states remain fixed during an episode. Yet reaching a goal may require opening a door to access a room and then opening a container to reveal a hidden target. We formulate interactive navigation beyond reachability, unifying channel-induced changes in spatial connectivity, container-induced changes in target visibility, and their sequential dependencies. We introduce MultINav-Bench, a 3K-episode ObjectGoal benchmark in MolmoSpaces spanning channel, container, and mixed tasks, with interaction-necessity annotations covering required and unnecessary episodes. Its data generation pipeline automates task construction across multiple task types. The evaluation protocol separately measures navigation success, interaction effectiveness, and cost. We further propose MultINav-Agent, a three-stage agentic framework built on a persistent, state-conditioned interaction graph. Semantic interactive perception grounds entities and interaction states; a vision-language model selects task-relevant actions based on expected changes in connectivity and target observability; and post-interaction verification updates the graph to guide subsequent navigation, exploration, or interaction. Experiments on MultINav-Bench show that MultINav-Agent consistently outperforms adapted baselines across channel, container, and mixed tasks.

</details>


### [155] [D-World: An Explicit World-Language-Action Model with Recursive Self-Improvement under Dual Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17114)
*Xuan Wen,Baichao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出D-World，一种显式的"世界-语言-行动"模型，通过感知、规划、行动三阶段分解，让LLM在冻结状态下借助可执行的数字孪生和基于测量的递归自我改进（RSI）循环来完成机器人任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿LLM已能较好地推理空间并编程机器人，但机器人策略背后的世界模型是隐式的（存于网络权重或预测视频中），无法被直接检查或纠正。作者希望构建一个可检查、可修正的显式世界模型。

Method: 基于Sense、Plan、Act分解：感知智能体用生成的视觉与几何代码将场景写成可执行数字孪生；规划智能体在仿真中搜索动作，并在每个动作块执行后，依据机器人实测数据修正孪生中的物体状态与具名物理参数。参数修正仅在重放最新动作块时所有测量残差均不超出容差才被接受，每个动作块运行前都在更新后的孪生中检查；结构性不匹配则返回感知重建。作者将"既推进任务又暴露模型误差"的动作选择建模为对偶控制问题。

Result: 在双臂机器人上，毛巾和吐司任务展示了局部修正的效果，例如某次修正将面包的渲染深度残差从22.4 mm降至1.5 mm，使执行无需重建即可继续。该循环随推理速度提升而加速。

Conclusion: 由于感知与规划仅通过孪生和语言通信，二者均可被学习模型替换。含证据成本决定了探测是否划算，因此廉价的局部修正可抵消谨慎控制器的停摆倾向。每段episode运行real-to-sim-to-real循环，其记录的孪生可离线重拟合与重渲染为更高保真环境用于后训练。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) now reason about space well enough to program robots, yet the world models behind robot policies are implicit, held in network weights or predicted video, and cannot be inspected or corrected directly. We present D-World, an explicit world-language-action model built on the Sense, Plan and Act decomposition. A perception agent writes the scene as an executable digital twin using generated vision and geometry code. A planning agent searches for actions in simulation and, after each executed action chunk, revises the twin's object states and named physical parameters against what the robot measured. A parameter revision is accepted only if replaying the latest chunk does not increase any measured residual beyond its tolerance, and every chunk is checked in the updated twin before it runs. Structural mismatches return to perception for reconstruction. We call this measurement-grounded cycle of correction and revision recursive self-improvement (RSI), in which the LLM stays frozen while the world it acts in improves. We model the choice of actions that both advance the task and expose model error as a dual control problem and show that the cost of incorporating evidence into the twin sets the threshold at which probing pays, so cheap local revision counteracts the turn-off of cautious controllers. Each episode runs a real-to-sim-to-real loop, and its recorded twins can be refitted and re-rendered offline into higher-fidelity environments for post-training. Because perception and planning communicate only through the twin and language, either can be replaced by a learned model, and since inference dominates the loop, the loop speeds up with faster models. On a dual-arm robot, towel and toaster tasks show local revisions, such as one reducing the bread's rendered-depth residual from 22.4 to 1.5 mm, that let execution continue without reconstruction.

</details>


### [156] [Force-Modulated Dexterity: Unified Compliance for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17161)
*Ying Yuan,David Held,Jitendra Malik,Haozhi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FOMO-Dex 统一柔顺性框架，在任务几何与接触结构定义的坐标下进行力驱动的运动修正，提升接触丰富操作的遥操作数据采集质量与策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作常用于收集机器人学习的高质量示教数据，但灵巧操作需要运动与接触力的协同控制。操作者能指定运动，却难以在不感知机器人接触力的情况下复现人类操作中细微的力调整，导致刚硬控制下接触力过大、数据质量受限。

Method: FOMO-Dex 是一种统一的柔顺性（compliance）表述：将名义动作表达在由任务几何与接触结构定义的坐标中，沿选定方向施加力驱动的运动修正，同时保持其余指令分量不变。

Result: 在取卡、剪纸、拧螺丝、螺母螺栓装配四类任务中，相比刚性控制，FOMO-Dex 降低了过大的接触力。用户研究表明操作者经简短练习后每小时可收集更多成功示教数据。策略学习上，模仿修正后的动作比模仿原始操作指令在取卡任务成功率更高；基于 FOMO-Dex 示教训练的策略在螺母螺栓和拧螺丝任务中比刚性控制器演示训练的策略实现更多旋转。

Conclusion: 基于任务几何与接触结构的力调制柔顺控制能有效改善接触丰富操作的遥操作数据采集与后续策略学习性能。

Abstract: Teleoperation is widely used to collect high-quality demonstrations for robot learning. Dexterous manipulation, however, requires coordinated control of motion and contact forces. Operators can specify motion, but struggle to reproduce the subtle force adjustments of human manipulation without feeling the robot's contacts. We introduce Force-Modulated Dexterity (FOMO-Dex), a unified compliance formulation for contact-rich manipulation. FOMO-Dex expresses nominal actions in coordinates defined by task geometry and contact structure. It applies force-driven motion corrections along selected directions while leaving the remaining command components unchanged. Across card picking, paper cutting, screwdriving, and nut-and-bolt assembly, FOMO-Dex reduces excessive contact forces compared with stiff control. A user study shows that operators collect more successful demonstration data per hour after brief practice. For policy learning, imitating the corrected actions improves card-picking success over imitating raw operator commands. Policies trained on FOMO-Dex demonstrations also achieve more rotation in nut-and-bolt manipulation and screwdriving than those trained on demonstrations collected with the stiff controller. Videos are shown on https://yingyuan0414.github.io/fomo-dex/ .

</details>


### [157] [Generative Flow on Symbolic Abstractions for Embodiment Capability Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17174)
*Youwei Yu,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FOSA框架，通过将抽象策略参数化为生成流网络（GFN）来自动构建符号化任务骨架，并以底层策略学习表现作为奖励信号，发现符合机器人具体形态能力边界的多样化可执行任务，进而蒸馏为语言条件策略以提升复杂长时程任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂、长时程机器人行为难以实现，因为成功执行依赖于机器人自身物理可供性所支撑的技能。现有训练任务分布多为手工设计或从大语言模型中采样，依赖语义合理性而非具体具身可行性，导致课程过难、冗余，或忽视非显而易见但可学习的行为。

Method: FOSA将抽象策略参数化为生成流网络，用于构建符号化任务骨架；每个骨架被编译为仿真环境，底层策略学习表现作为抽象策略的奖励信号，从而学习一个"有根基"的任务生成器。

Result: 在多种异构机器人平台上，FOSA发现了多样且可执行的、接近机器人经验能力前沿的任务；将发现的任务策略蒸馏为单一语言条件策略后，在未见过的复杂及长时程任务上性能得到提升。

Conclusion: 通过将任务生成与具身可行性挂钩，FOSA能够自动发现符合机器人能力边界的有效课程，并提升语言条件策略在复杂长时程任务上的泛化能力。代码与演示视频已公开。

Abstract: Achieving complex, long-horizon robot behavior remains challenging because successful execution depends on skills grounded in a robot's physical affordances. Existing training-task distributions are often handcrafted or sampled from large language models (LLMs), relying on semantic plausibility rather than embodiment-specific feasibility. This mismatch can produce curricula that are too difficult, redundant, or blind to non-obvious but learnable behaviors. We propose Generative Flow on Symbolic Abstractions (FOSA), a framework for discovering embodiment-specific capabilities by learning a grounded task generator. FOSA parameterizes an abstract policy as a Generative Flow Network that constructs symbolic task skeletons. Each skeleton is compiled into a simulation environment, where low-level policy-learning performance provides the reward signal for the abstract policy. Across heterogeneous robot platforms, FOSA discovers diverse and executable tasks near the robot's empirical capability frontier. After distilling the discovered task policies into a single language-conditioned policy, FOSA improves performance on unseen complex and long-horizon tasks. The code and demonstration videos are available at https://www.youwei-yu.com/fosa.

</details>


### [158] [DexNinja: Learning Robust Dexterous Cutting Policy with a Real-to-Sim-to-Real Data Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17208)
*Haozhe Lou,Runyi Yang,Wanqi Zhong,Caiyun Liu,Yicheng Liu,Wenhao Liu,Wenlong Ma,Jiawei Xia,Kanzhi Wu,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Hang Zhao,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexNinja是一个real2sim2real框架，用少量真实示范生成稠密训练分布，在灵巧切食物任务上实现超过60%的成功率并具备泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人切割食物是高度接触的交互，涉及可变形、弹塑性物体，其行为随姿态、接触模式和切割阶段变化。真实数据采集昂贵且浪费（每次试验会污染或损坏食物），难以扩展；仿真虽是可替代方案，但切割这类拓扑变化交互在物理与感知上存在严重的sim-to-real差距。

Method: DexNinja框架包含三步：(i) 从真实采集重建物体实例；(ii) 在类别级约束下随机化物理意义明确的参数；(iii) 使用定制的可微仿真器（耦合机器人动力学、触觉接触与可变形切割）生成增强的灵巧操作回合。

Result: 在灵巧切食物任务上：(1) 用数十条真实示范和数百条增强仿真数据，成功率可达60%以上；(2) 仅用真实数据时通过sim-to-real，增强数据可将成功率提升20%以上；(3) 所得策略可泛化到形状和尺寸不同的分布外物体。

Conclusion: DexNinja通过real2sim2real方式有效缩小拓扑变化切割任务的sim-to-real差距，用少量真实数据加仿真增强即获得高性能且可泛化的灵巧切割策略。

Abstract: Cutting a piece of food looks effortless in human hands. For a robot, however, every cut is a high-contact interaction with a deformable, elasto-plastic object whose behavior changes with pose, contact mode, and cutting stage. Collecting real-world data is expensive and wasteful: each trial typically contaminates or damages the food, so obtaining scalable real world data for dexterous cutting is expensive and difficult. Simulation is an appealing alternative, but a large sim-to-real gap exists in both physics and perception. This gap is especially severe for topology-changing interactions like cutting, where small errors in contact, friction, or material and elasticity responses quickly compound. We propose \textsc{DexNinja}, a real2sim2real framework that turns a handful of real demonstrations into a dense and realistic training distribution. \textsc{DexNinja} (i) reconstructs object instances from real captures, (ii) randomizes physically meaningful parameters under category-level constraints, and (iii) produces augmented dexterous manipulation episodes using a custom differentiable simulator that couples robot dynamics, tactile contact, and deformable cutting. We evaluate \textsc{DexNinja} on a dexterous food-slicing task and show that (1) with tens of real demonstrations and hundreds of augmented simulator data, the success rate could reach more than 60\%. (2) the augmented data consistently improves success rates when only using real data with more than 20\% via sim-to-real, and (3) the resulting policies generalize to out-of-distribution objects with varying shapes and sizes.

</details>


### [159] [LiDAR-based Humanoid Navigation in Dynamic, Non-Convex Environments via Spatio-Temporal Safe Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17232)
*Shubham S. Kumbhar,Panagiotis Artemiadis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个实时人形机器人导航框架，从LiDAR粒子表示直接构建时空安全走廊，并结合LIP规划与全身控制，在动态非凸环境中实现稳健导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态、非凸环境中的人形机器人导航需要紧耦合机载感知、落脚点规划与动态运动控制。现有方法常通过简化凸障碍边界丢弃复杂自由空间，且未考虑实时动态占据预测，导致在移动非凸结构中导航受限。

Method: 从基于LiDAR的粒子表示直接构建时空安全走廊，避免限制性几何假设；将走廊与基于线性倒立摆（LIP）的规划及全身控制（WBC）耦合；采用迭代多面体生成方法。

Result: 在Digit人形机器人的仿真中，于24×24 m²场地内绕行及进入移动非凸障碍的成功率达96%；消融研究确认迭代多面体生成在狭窄走廊中显著优于单次迭代基线；评估显示对杂乱、传感器噪声、控制延迟和力扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架可在实时条件下处理移动非凸结构，无需限制性几何假设，并展现出高成功率与强鲁棒性，验证了时空安全走廊与LIP/WBC紧耦合的有效性。

Abstract: Navigating humanoid robots through dynamic, non-convex environments requires tightly coupling onboard perception with footstep planning and dynamic locomotion control. Existing methods frequently discard complex free space through simplified convex obstacle bounds and fail to account for real-time dynamic occupancy predictions. This paper presents a real-time framework for humanoid navigation that directly constructs spatio-temporal safe corridors from LiDAR-based particle representations and couples them with Linear Inverted Pendulum (LIP)-based planning and Whole Body Control (WBC) to enable navigation around and within moving non-convex structures without restrictive geometric assumptions. Simulations on the Digit humanoid yield a 96% success rate in navigating around and inside moving non-convex obstacles in a 24 by 24 m^2 arena. Ablation studies confirm that the proposed iterative polytope generation significantly outperforms single-iteration baselines in narrow corridors, while evaluations demonstrate robustness to clutter, sensor noise, control delays, and force perturbations.

</details>


### [160] [Value-Preserving Action Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17252)
*Max Muchen Sun,Jueun Kwon,Benjamin Riviere,Todd Murphey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出值保持动作量化（VAQ），通过保持状态可达性来量化动作，并将其与MCTS结合（VAQ-MCTS）用于行为克隆，在物体推动任务中实现了高效、鲁棒且可零样本适应的策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于搜索的策略学习（如MCTS）能以运行时间换取数据效率和适应新环境的能力。但在机器人学习中，应用搜索需要对连续动作空间及动力学进行离散化（通常通过动作量化）。而基于重建的量化虽能表示连续动作空间，却不足以满足策略学习的需求——策略学习还要求量化后的动作能保持其对未来状态和价值的影响。作者指出，这种需求并不能由动作重建误差所捕捉。

Method: 作者从理论上证明：由动作量化引起的价值函数误差，可由量化动作下状态可达性的重建误差严格界定。基于此结论，提出值保持动作量化（VAQ），即通过保持状态可达性来量化动作。进一步将VAQ与MCTS结合，形成VAQ-MCTS，作为值保持的、基于搜索的行为克隆策略学习框架。

Result: 在一系列物体推动任务作为受控基准上，将VAQ-MCTS与最先进的行为克隆方法进行比较。VAQ-MCTS在标准设置下达到或超越其性能，同时大幅提升数据效率和对异构演示的鲁棒性，并支持对新环境的零样本适应。硬件实验进一步验证了VAQ-MCTS的有效性，仅用10分钟演示数据即完成了全部30次真实世界物体推动试验。

Conclusion: VAQ-MCTS是一种值保持的、基于搜索的行为克隆框架，其核心在于以保持状态可达性为目标进行动作量化，从而兼顾数据效率、鲁棒性与零样本适应能力，并在真实机器人任务中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Search-based policy learning, such as Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS), offers the flexibility to trade runtime computation for data efficiency and adaptation to novel environments. For robot learning, however, applying search requires discretizing the continuous action space and associated dynamics, typically through action quantization. While reconstruction-based quantization is sufficient for representing the continuous action space alone, policy learning additionally requires the quantized actions to preserve their effects on future states and values. We show that such requirement is not necessarily captured by action reconstruction error. Instead, we prove that the error in the value function resulting from action quantization is tightly bounded by the reconstruction error of state reachability under the quantized actions. Based on these results, we propose value-preserving action quantization (VAQ), which quantizes actions by preserving state reachability. We further combine VAQ with MCTS (VAQ-MCTS) as a value-preserving, search-based policy learning framework for behavioral cloning. Using a wide range of object pushing tasks as a controlled benchmark, we evaluate VAQ-MCTS against state-of-the-art behavioral cloning methods. VAQ-MCTS matches or exceeds their performance under standard settings, while substantially improving data efficiency and robustness to heterogeneous demonstrations, while enabling zero-shot adaptation to novel environments. Hardware experiments further demonstrate the effectiveness of VAQ-MCTS, completing all 30 real-world object pushing trials with only 10 minutes of demonstration data.

</details>


### [161] [Physical SLAM: Physics-aware Simultaneous Localisation and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17260)
*Guangming Wang,Zhishuo Liu,Haibing Wu,Wanli Ma,Yixiong Jing,Olaf Wysocki,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Physical SLAM，一种物理感知的 4D 高斯场重建方法，可从单目 RGB-D 视频中重建可变形物体并预测其在外力下的未来状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从移动相机单目视频重建物理世界预测性 4D 表示具有挑战性，需同时重建动态高斯辐射场并推断其物理动力学，现有方法缺乏物理感知能力。

Method: 将场景分为静态背景高斯场（用于相机定位）和动态物体高斯场；动态场由弹簧-质量物理模型驱动（含弹性变形、惯性、阻尼）。引入虚拟观测力将仿真表面节点逐渐拉向观测深度点；物理参数从整个重建观测窗口的仿真误差聚合更新。设计物理感知高斯变换与表面对齐策略，使动态高斯沿弹簧-质量模型变形表面变换并在渲染优化中保持对齐。

Result: 在 PhysTwin 基准上，重建与预测 PSNR 分别比 PhysTwin 提升 10.4% 和 11.0%；在 TUM RGB-D 基准上，相比 4DGS-SLAM 平均相机跟踪误差降低 20.6%，SSIM 提升 3.6%，LPIPS 降低 8.3%。

Conclusion: 该方法支持观测帧渲染与动作条件仿真，将 SLAM 地图扩展到描述可变形物体对操作响应，在重建、预测与跟踪精度上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: To reconstruct a predictive 4D representation of the physical world from monocular video captured by a moving camera, we jointly reconstruct dynamic Gaussian radiance fields and infer their underlying physical dynamics. This paper proposes \emph{Physical SLAM}, a physics-aware method for constructing 4D Gaussian fields of deformable objects and predicting their future state when subjected to external actions, given a sequence of RGB-D images. Our method separates the scene into a static background Gaussian field for camera localization and a dynamic object Gaussian field driven by a spring--mass physical model of elastic deformation, inertia, and damping. To integrate visual observations with physical dynamics, we introduce virtual observation forces that gradually pull simulated surface nodes toward observed depth points, avoiding abrupt replacement of simulated node positions. Meanwhile, the physical parameters of the spring--mass model are updated from simulation errors aggregated over the full reconstruction observation window, enabling the reconstructed dynamics to align with observations. To couple appearance, geometry, and physics, we further design a physics-aware Gaussian transformation and surface alignment strategy, where dynamic Gaussians are transformed along the deforming physical surface of the spring--mass model and constrained to remain aligned with the surface during rendering-based optimization. The resulting representation supports observed-frame rendering and action-conditioned simulation, extending SLAM maps to describe how deformable objects respond to manipulation. On the PhysTwin benchmark, our method improves reconstruction and prediction PSNR over PhysTwin by 10.4\% and 11.0\%, respectively. On the TUM RGB-D benchmark, it reduces average camera tracking error by 20.6\%, improves SSIM by 3.6\%, and reduces LPIPS by 8.3\% compared with 4DGS-SLAM.

</details>


### [162] [ReForesight: Occlusion-Robust Re-Identification and Future Occupancy Forecasting for Dynamic Semantic Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17279)
*Didar Seyidov,Faith Ogunwoye,Hajira Amjad,Danil Tolkachev,Yuri Sarkisov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReForesight是一个开源ROS 2工具包，在粒子式语义建图流程中加入实例级未来占用预测与遮挡后重识别，用36.6k参数MLP预测行人和车辆未来3秒位置，无需占用解码器即可生成"幽灵"点云。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在共享步道上行走时需要知道人的未来位置，但语义占用地图只显示当前所在位置，缺乏对未来占用的预测能力，也无法在遮挡后重新关联目标。

Method: 采用36.6k参数的MLP对每个被跟踪的人或车辆预测未来最多3秒位置，辅以卡尔曼回退与静止门控保护；将目标自身点云按预测位姿移动，在建图坐标系中形成"幽灵"点云，无需占用解码器；可选跟踪器利用流程已计算的关键点描述子和度量3D门控在遮挡后重新关联目标。

Result: 在四个留出的CODa序列上，MLP将平均3秒最终位移误差从0.728米（卡尔曼）降至0.662米，并在每个序列上优于同等规模的GRU（0.692米），单CPU核心上每实例耗时0.04毫秒。

Conclusion: ReForesight以极轻量模型实现了实例级未来占用预测与遮挡后重识别，代码、全部模型和无ROS演示均已开源，并在户外清洁机器人上完成现场验证。

Abstract: Robots sharing walkways need to know where people will be, yet semantic occupancy maps show only where they are. We present ReForesight, an open-source ROS 2 toolkit that adds instance-level future occupancy and re-identification after occlusion to a particle-based semantic mapping pipeline. A 36.6k-parameter MLP predicts each tracked person or vehicle up to 3 s ahead, guarded by a Kalman fallback and a stationary gate, and the object's own points are moved to the forecast pose, forming a "ghost" point cloud in the map frame without an occupancy decoder. An optional tracker re-associates objects after occlusion using keypoint descriptors the pipeline already computes and a metric 3D gate. On four held-out CODa sequences, the MLP lowers the mean 3 s final displacement error from 0.728 m (Kalman) to 0.662 m and beats a similar-size GRU (0.692 m) on every sequence, at 0.04 ms per instance on one CPU core. Code, all models, and a ROS-free demo are released; field demonstrations use an outdoor cleaning robot.

</details>


### [163] [Phase-Conditioned Affordance Fields for SE(3)-Equivariant Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17281)
*Xinzhe Chen,Liqi Huang,Sihua Ren,Haowen Sun,Mingyang Li,Zeyang Liu,Xingyu Chen,Xuguang Lan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出相位条件可供性场（PAF），通过在点云上学习逐点分数来引入局部几何归纳偏置，在保留全局SE(3)等变性的同时提升视觉运动策略在操作任务中的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SE(3)等变视觉运动策略基于全局等变假设，即把整个观测视为受单一刚体变换支配。但真实操作任务中，任务相关区域会随不同物体而变化，导致该假设失效，因此需要一种能捕捉局部几何结构的等变策略。

Method: 在观测点云上学习相位条件可供性场（PAF），即每个点的分数，并以两种互补方式使用：一是以分数加权质心作为学习到的、相位条件的锚点，将观测与动作围绕活跃区域进行规范化，替代固定的夹爪坐标系参考；二是将分数作为逐点特征附加。这样引入局部几何归纳偏置，同时保持策略的全局SE(3)等变性。

Result: 在多个MimicGen任务及不同空间随机化下验证，PAF持续提升底层策略的成功率；在多物体真实机器人任务中，PAF优于基线，并在光照变化下保持领先。

Conclusion: PAF通过相位条件可供性场为SE(3)等变策略引入局部归纳偏置，克服了全局等变假设的局限，在仿真与真实机器人任务中均有效，并对光照变化具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: SE(3)-equivariance provides a useful geometric inductive bias for visuomotor policies, encoding that actions covary with rigid transformations of the observation. However, existing SE(3)-equivariant visuomotor policies build this bias on a global equivariance assumption that treats the entire observation as governed by a single rigid transformation, which breaks down in real-world manipulation tasks where the task-relevant region changes across different objects. We address this by learning a phase-conditioned affordance field (PAF) over the observed point cloud, a per-point score that the SE(3)-equivariant policy uses in two complementary ways. Its score-weighted centroid serves as a learned, phase-conditioned anchor that canonicalizes the observation and the action around the active region, replacing the fixed gripper-frame reference, and the score itself is appended as a per-point feature. This introduces a local geometric inductive bias, while preserving the policy's global SE(3) equivariance. We validate on multiple MimicGen tasks under varying spatial randomization, and PAF consistently improves the success rate of the underlying policy. On multi-object real-robot tasks, PAF outperforms baselines and keeps its lead under lighting variations.

</details>


### [164] [Improving Policy Learning from Delayed Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17298)
*Xiru Zhu,David Meger,Gregory Dudek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究人类监督式机器人模仿学习中的"专家干预延迟"问题，发现延迟干预会导致学习效果变差，并提出 LfDI 方法，通过学习代价函数来推断额外监督信号，从而在仿真环境中优于未考虑延迟的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往模仿学习研究主要关注"次优专家"（专家策略本身会犯错）的影响，但普遍假设专家干预在错误发生后立即进行。现实中人类监督者的干预往往存在延迟，而延迟干预对策略学习的影响尚未被系统研究。尽管延迟干预可视为一种噪声、理论上可能提升鲁棒性，但作者发现它实际上会削弱学习效果，尤其是难以展示"错误规避"行为，因此需要专门的方法来应对。

Method: 提出 Learning from Delayed Intervention（LfDI）。该方法基于专家在"何处"和"何时"进行干预的信息，学习一个代价函数；同时结合专家提供的关于干预延迟程度的有限信息。学到的代价函数被用来为没有专家数据的状态推断额外的监督信号，从而引导策略避开那些很可能触发专家干预的状态。

Result: 实验表明：从延迟专家干预中学习，总体上不如从无延迟专家干预中学习有效；延迟干预在展示错误规避行为方面效果更差。而所提出的 LfDI 方法在仿真环境中评估，性能优于其他未考虑干预延迟的方法。

Conclusion: 专家干预延迟是模仿学习中一个被忽视但重要的问题，它会削弱错误规避行为的学习。通过建模干预的时空信息并学习代价函数来补充监督信号，LfDI 能有效缓解延迟带来的负面影响，在仿真环境中取得了优于不考虑延迟的方法的表现。

Abstract: In this work, we explore the idea of expert intervention delays in human-supervised robotic systems for imitation learning system. Previous works in imitation learning have primarily examined the impact of suboptimal experts, where the expert policy makes mistakes, but assumed expert interventions occur immediately after errors are made. Although delayed expert intervention can be viewed as a form of noise that might improve policy robustness, our results show that learning from delayed expert interventions is overall less effective than learning from non-delayed expert interventions; delayed expert interventions are less effective at demonstrating error avoidance behaviors.
  To address this, we propose Learning from Delayed Intervention (LfDI), which learns a cost function based on where and when the expert intervenes, combined with limited information about the extent of intervention delays provided by the expert. This cost function is then used to infer additional supervision for states without expert data, guiding the policy to avoid states that would likely trigger interventions. We evaluate our method, Learning from Delayed Intervention (LfDI), on simulated environments and show that it outperforms other methods that do not account for intervention delays.

</details>


### [165] [Evaluating Long-Horizon Robot Planning under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17326)
*Harsh Muriki,Gayathri Aravindan,Yuchen Zheng,Sonia Chernova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在Habitat中构建了包含七种长时程家庭任务的基准，通过改变机器人初始记忆与环境配置，在统一执行框架下评测ReAct、VLM-TAMP和Tru-POMDP的适应性，共完成90个任务-环境-记忆组合、1,350个episode。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人需要在内部模型和外部世界都不完美的条件下成功导航与执行任务，因此需要系统评测不同规划器在记忆不准确与环境不确定性下的表现。

Method: 基于Habitat构建七个长时程家庭任务基准，变化初始机器人记忆与环境配置；在共享执行框架下适配并比较ReAct、VLM-TAMP和Tru-POMDP，覆盖90个任务-环境-记忆组合，共1,350个episode；评测任务成功率、部分完成度、动作效率，并对执行轨迹进行规则化分析。

Result: ReAct取得最高总体任务成功率（45.3%）和完成度（71.7%）；初始记忆准确并不总是带来最高性能；轨迹分析发现反复出现的放置错误、前置条件错误和冗余动作。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了不同规划器在利用初始知识与应对不确定性方面的差异，并表明初始记忆质量与规划性能之间并非简单正相关。

Abstract: Household robots must successfully navigate im-perfect circumstances, both in their internal models and the external world. We introduce a benchmark of seven long-horizon household tasks in Habitat that vary initial robot memory and environment configurations. We evaluate adaptations of ReAct, VLM-TAMP, and Tru-POMDP within a shared execution framework across 90 tasks-environment-memory combinations, totaling 1,350 episodes. Our evaluation measures task success, partial completion, and action efficiency, along with rule-based analysis of execution traces. ReAct achieves the highest overall task success (45.3%) and completion (71.7%), while having accurate initial memory does not consistently yield the highest performance for every planner. We identify recurring placement errors, precondition errors, and redundant actions through trace analysis. These findings highlight differences in how the evaluated planners use initial knowledge and respond to uncertainty.

</details>


### [166] [Can a Frontier VLM Serve as a Robot Policy for Memory-Dependent Manipulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17350)
*Jack B. Jedlicki,Amish Sethi,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 冻结的前沿视觉语言模型 GPT-6-Astra 直接作为机器人策略，在 RoboMME 基准上取得 92.1% 成功率，超过此前人类分数（90.5%，且使用 oracle 技能接口），远超最佳榜单成绩（60.8%）和分层系统（79.1%），16 项任务中 13 项追平或超过人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通用视觉语言模型能否在具身系统中同时接管长时程规划、记忆与末端执行器控制，从而模糊传统模块化分工的边界。

Method: 将冻结的 GPT-6-Astra 直接用作机器人策略：整段 episode 保持在上下文中，模型输出每个末端执行器航点与夹爪命令，不做任何 RoboMME 专属训练。在包含 16 项依赖记忆的操作任务（涵盖时序、空间、物体与程序性记忆）的 RoboMME 基准上，对 800 个测试 episode 进行评估，并与人类分数、榜单最佳条目及分层系统进行对比。

Result: 800 个测试 episode 成功率为 92.1%，高于此前使用 oracle 技能接口获得的人类分数 90.5%，远超最佳榜单条目 60.8% 和同模型搭配 RoboMME 训练策略的分层系统 79.1%。16 项任务中有 13 项追平或超过人类分数。63 次失败集中在两项任务，主要类型为过早完成以及时机错误或非预期的按钮按压。

Conclusion: 结果模糊了长时程规划、记忆与末端执行器控制之间通常的划分，并提出一个开放问题：在具身系统中，单一通用模型能接管这一技术栈的多少部分。

Abstract: We use a frozen frontier vision-language model, GPT-6-Astra, directly as the robot policy on RoboMME, a benchmark of 16 memory-dependent manipulation tasks spanning temporal, spatial, object, and procedural memory. The model keeps the whole episode in context and outputs every end-effector waypoint and gripper command, with no RoboMME-specific training. On the 800 test episodes it succeeds in 92.1%, above the previously reported human score of 90.5%, which was obtained with an oracle skill interface, and far ahead of the best leaderboard entry (60.8%) and of a hierarchical system that pairs the same model with a RoboMME-trained policy (79.1%). It matches or exceeds the human score on 13 of the 16 tasks. Its 63 failures concentrate in two tasks and are mostly premature completions and mistimed or unintended button presses. These results blur the usual division between long-horizon planning, memory and end-effector control, and raise the question of how much of this stack a single general-purpose model can take over in embodied systems. Project website: https://embodied-astra.github.io. Code, prompts and all episode logs: https://github.com/jackbjed/embodied-astra-robomme.

</details>


### [167] [ClearNav: Perception-Aligned Quadrotor Flight Using Differential-Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17419)
*Aditya Azad,Arthur Huang,Guanrui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ClearNav导航策略，通过两个约束朝向观测方向的奖励项与前向朝向目标，减少不朝相机观测方向的运动，从而降低杂乱环境中四旋翼的碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主四旋翼在杂乱环境中飞行对巡检、搜索等任务十分关键。仿真训练并迁移到硬件的导航策略虽能在开阔空间高速飞行，但障碍间距缩小时仍会碰撞。作者将这些碰撞主要归因于一个机制：朝机载相机未观测方向运动，包括朝向与行进方向不一致以及避障时的横向偏移，二者都会使飞行器驶向视野外障碍。

Method: ClearNav是一个导航策略，使用三个奖励项训练：前向朝向目标与横向速度惩罚，将运动约束在观测方向上；第三个项对靠近障碍时的速度施加惩罚，使进入视野的障碍仍可规避。

Result: 在仿真中，18个留出场景、每策略2304次配对飞行（训练未见）下，ClearNav相比现有最优方法成功率提升6.8个百分点，碰撞率降低65%，最高飞行速度5m/s。仅用接近惩罚项并在匹配指令速度下，碰撞率仍降低70%。效果在静态场景显著，在移动障碍上方向一致但整体不显著。并在真实杂乱环境中验证，四旋翼可成功安全穿行。

Conclusion: 即使在小型无人机的受限传感器与算力条件下，也能实现杂乱环境中更安全的飞行。

Abstract: Autonomous quadrotor flight in cluttered environment is an key capability for tasks like inspection and search. Navigation policies trained in simulation and transferred to hardware can fly at high speed in open space, but they can still collide as obstacle clearance shrinks. We attribute most of these collisions to a single mechanism, namely motion in directions the onboard camera does not observe. Heading that is not aligned with the direction of travel and lateral excursion during obstacle avoidance both carry the vehicle toward obstacles outside its field of view. We introduce ClearNav, a navigation policy trained with two reward terms that constrain motion to the observed direction, a forward-looking heading target and a penalty on lateral velocity. A third term penalizes speed near obstacles so that an obstacle entering the field of view remains avoidable. In simulation, across 18 held-out scenarios with 2304 paired flights per policy unseen in training, ClearNav improves success by 6.8 points, cuts the collision rate by 65% relative to the state of the arts, and flies at maximum 5m/s. For the proximity term alone under matched commanded speeds, the collision reduction still holds at 70%. The effect is significant on static scenes and consistent in direction on moving obstacles, though not significant there at the suite level, showing that safer flight in clutter is possible even on the limited sensors and compute of small drones. We also validate our navigation policy in a real-world cluttered environment, showing that it enables the quadrotor to navigate through the cluttered environment successfully and safely.

</details>


### [168] [MIRROR: Reflecting Hidden Symmetries from Offline Robot Interaction Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17422)
*Shiqi Liu,Yihang Yao,Peide Huang,Mengdi Xu,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIRROR 从离线交互数据中学习已知对称群在 POMDP 观测与动作上的联合变换，用于对称感知的机器人策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素观测下，即使简单的物理对称也会导致复杂、视角依赖的图像变换，难以手工定义；现有对称感知方法依赖预定义变换的表示，需要针对传感设置和任务进行预处理。

Method: 通过对抗式元组匹配在一步转移元组上耦合观测与动作变换，并用显式的群一致性正则化惩罚违反群运算公理的行为。

Result: 在四个 Robosuite 桌面操作任务上，四个对称质量指标中的三个取得最低总误差，其中兼容性误差较适配的 LaLiGAN 基线降低 61%；用学习到的变换做帧平均 BC，四个任务平均成功率从 80.48% 提升到 85.12%，单任务最高提升 9.66 个百分点；在高度图和分割掩码观测下重复发现与训练，并将策略零样本部署到真实 Kinova GEN3 机械臂。

Conclusion: MIRROR 无需针对传感设置手工设计变换，即可从离线数据学习观测-动作耦合对称，并提升策略性能且支持零样本真机部署。

Abstract: Symmetry-aware robot policy learning requires coupled observation-action transformations. For pixel observations, even simple physical symmetries can induce complex, viewpoint-dependent image transformations that are difficult to specify by hand. Existing symmetry-aware methods often rely on representations with predefined transformations, requiring preprocessing tailored to the sensing setup and task. We introduce MIRROR, a framework that learns how a known symmetry group operates jointly on observations and actions in partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) from offline interaction data. MIRROR couples these transformations through adversarial tuple matching over one-step transition tuples, while explicit group-consistency regularization penalizes violations of the group-operation axioms. On four Robosuite tabletop manipulation tasks, MIRROR achieves the lowest aggregate error on three of four symmetry-quality metrics, including a 61% reduction in compatibility error (which measures consistency under transformation composition) relative to an adapted LaLiGAN baseline. Using the learned transformations in frame-averaged behavior cloning (BC) yields higher mean success rates than vanilla BC on all four tasks, increasing average success from 80.48% to 85.12%, with per-task gains up to 9.66 percentage points. We also repeat symmetry discovery and policy training in simulation with heightmap and segmentation-mask observations, then deploy the resulting policy zero-shot on a real Kinova GEN3 arm. Project page: https://pomdp-mirror.github.io.

</details>


### [169] [Smoothness Ranks Skill, Not Success: An Audit of Published Trajectory-Smoothness Curation Metrics Under Episode-Length Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17427)
*Jaiveer Bassi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究在真实 robomimic 数据上检验了 rinse 提出的轨迹平滑度指标（SAL、TED），发现它们无法在控制回合长度后保持有效性，TED 甚至产生反效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹平滑度被提议为一种低成本、免训练的信号，用于筛选模仿学习演示，最近的 rinse 提出了 SAL 和 TED 两项指标。作者想验证这些已发表的指标在真实数据上是否经得起回合长度的混淆检验。

Method: 在 robomimic 真实数据（can/mh、square/mh、can/mg）上，对比 SAL、TED、原始平均 jerk 与纯回合长度的 AUROC，并在时长匹配区间内重新评估；随后用 GMM 行为克隆（10 个种子）与扩散策略，分别按各指标筛选的子集训练，对比下游成功率。

Result: can/mh 上 SAL 的 AUROC 为 0.952，但回合长度单独达 0.957，时长匹配后 SAL 降至 0.628，而 jerk 升至 0.896；square/mh 上混淆重现。can/mg（全部 150 步）上 SAL 仅 0.454、TED 反向为 0.376，jerk 为 0.706。GMM 训练中按 TED 筛选比随机选择差约三分之一（0.240 vs. 0.360，p=0.0001，通过 Bonferroni 校正）；SAL 与 jerk 与不筛选无异。扩散策略下 TED 仍最差（五种子下不显著），jerk 显著优于两个基线，说明筛选结论不能跨策略配置迁移。

Conclusion: 已发布的平滑度指标（尤其 TED）在控制回合长度后不再可靠，甚至反向预测任务成功，其效果受数据集与策略配置影响，不能直接迁移使用。

Abstract: Trajectory smoothness has been proposed as a cheap, training-free signal for curating imitation-learning demonstrations, most recently by rinse, which introduces Spectral Arc Length (SAL) and a contact-aware Trajectory-Envelope Distance (TED). We test whether these published metrics survive episode-length controls on real robomimic data. On can/mh, SAL separates better from worse operators with AUROC 0.952, but episode length alone achieves 0.957, and inside a duration-matched band SAL falls to 0.628 while raw mean jerk, the baseline rinse argues against, rises to 0.896. The confound replicates on square/mh. On can/mg, where every episode is exactly 150 steps, SAL is at chance (0.454) and TED is inverted (0.376) against task success, while jerk reaches 0.706. Training GMM behavior-cloning policies on each metric's curated subset (10 seeds), curating by TED costs roughly a third of downstream success relative to random selection of the same size (0.240 vs. 0.360, p=0.0001, surviving Bonferroni correction), while SAL and jerk are indistinguishable from not curating. Under a diffusion policy TED still ranks worst, though its deficit is not resolvable at five seeds, and jerk becomes significantly better than both baselines, so the curation decision does not transfer unchanged across the two policy configurations we tested. Inspecting trajectories shows why TED inverts: successful manipulation requires sustained high-deviation motion through contact, while failure is low-amplitude hovering that a smoothed envelope follows closely.

</details>


### [170] [Act4R: Action-conditioned 4D reconstruction for kinematics-free control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17462)
*Zhaoliang Zhang,Chenhao Yu,Zhimin Shao,Xijun Liu,Cheng Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Act4R 利用3D基础模型预测动作条件下的机器人3D场景流，并在场景流预测与逆向控制任务上超越此前最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨多种机器人本体的精确运动控制长期具有挑战性：对复杂机器人结构建模通常需要大量时间、专用传感器和人工标注。论文希望探索能否利用从2D观测提升得到的4D表征，基于动作信号密集地映射机器人的空间运动，从而减少对建模与标注的依赖。

Method: 提出Act4R框架，基于3D基础模型预测给定动作下机器人的3D场景流。其核心是一个空间-动作编码器（Spatial-Action Encoder），将当前2D机器人状态与动作信号编码为表征未来3D状态的嵌入；随后将当前与未来空间嵌入解码为点图，以估计动作条件下的场景流。为有效学习动作到未来状态的映射，在基于Transformer的编码器中引入空间-动作融合，并设计空间对齐损失引导编码器进行未来流预测。在给定本体的少量样本上训练后，Act4R可通过优化信号实现逆向控制，达到期望的场景流。

Result: 在公开真实数据集与仿真器采集的合成数据上进行评估。多种机器人平台上的实验表明，Act4R在场景流预测上优于此前SoTA；在逆向控制上，无论动作精度指标还是仿真闭环控制，均表现更优。

Conclusion: Act4R通过将3D基础模型与空间-动作融合结合，实现了动作条件下的3D场景流预测，并可支持逆向控制，验证了利用4D表征进行机器人运动建模的有效性。

Abstract: Precise control of movement across various robotic embodiments is a longstanding challenge. Modeling complex robotic structures requires significant time, dedicated sensors, and manual labeling. Recent advancements in 4D representation learning offer an interesting alternative: can we leverage 4D features lifted from 2D observations to densely map a robot's spatial movement based on the action signal? To this end, we propose Act4R, a framework that builds on 3D foundation model to predict a robot's 3D scene flow with any given action. Act4R introduces a Spatial-Action Encoder, which encodes the current 2D robot state and its action signals into embeddings that represent future robot state in 3D. Then, both the current and future spatial embeddings are decoded into point maps to estimate action-conditioned scene flow. To effectively learn the mapping between action and future state, Act4R introduces spatial-action fusion in its transformer-based encoder, and a spatial alignment loss that guides the encoder for future flow prediction. After training on some samples of a given embodiment, Act4R can perform inverse control by optimizing the signal to achieve the desired scene flow. We evaluate Act4R on a public real-world dataset and synthetic data collected in simulator. Experiments on various robotic platforms show that Act4R outperforms the previous SoTA in scene flow prediction. Our method also performs better in inverse control, both on the action accuracy metric and in closed-loop control in simulation.

</details>


### [171] [From Orbit Matching to Docking: A Hierarchical Reinforcement-Learning Approach to Multiphase Rendezvous Trajectory Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17468)
*Mariko Storey-Matsutani,Anatoly Zlotnik,Lee Holguin,Zigfried Hampel-Arias,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个分层强化学习框架，用于自主完成从轨道匹配到对接的完整交会与接近操作任务，通过训练五个阶段专用策略并由一个学习到的高层管理器策略动态选择控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间任务复杂度提升，自主制导需应对不同任务阶段间动力学、目标与约束的切换。交会与接近操作（RPO）跨越多个操作区间，现有强化学习方法多仅处理其中一部分序列，或依赖人工定义的控制器切换条件，难以覆盖完整轨迹。

Method: 提出分层强化学习框架，针对三自由度RPO任务训练五个专用策略，分别对应轨道匹配、远距离接近、中距离接近、近距离接近和对接；再由一个学习到的高层管理器策略根据航天器状态在这些阶段控制器间进行选择。在完整端到端交会序列上进行评估，并与固定顺序调用启发式方法对比。

Result: 在完整端到端交会序列上评估了该分层方法，并与固定顺序调用启发式方法进行了对比，以评估轨迹性能和阶段过渡行为；同时分析了各阶段策略及整个分层系统的训练与推理指标。

Conclusion: 分层强化学习框架能够自主覆盖从轨道匹配到对接的完整RPO过程，通过学习的管理器策略实现阶段间切换，为多阶段空间任务自主制导提供了可行途径。

Abstract: As the complexity of space missions increases, autonomous spacecraft guidance must accommodate transitions between mission phases with distinct dynamics, objectives, and constraints. Rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) are particularly challenging because they span multiple operating regimes, from orbital-scale matching and far-range approach to close-range maneuvering and terminal docking. Existing reinforcement-learning approaches generally address only a subset of this sequence or rely on manually defined switching conditions between controllers. This study presents a hierarchical reinforcement-learning framework for autonomous, three-degree-of-freedom RPO that spans the complete trajectory from orbit matching to docking. Five specialized policies are trained for orbit matching, far-range approach, mid-range approach, close-range approach, and docking. A learned high-level manager policy then selects among these phase-specific controllers based on the evolving spacecraft state. The hierarchical approach is evaluated over the full end-to-end rendezvous sequence and compared with a fixed sequential-invocation heuristic to assess trajectory performance and transition behavior. Training and inference metrics are also analyzed for the individual phase policies and the complete hierarchical system.

</details>


### [172] [An Optimal Algorithm for Changing from Longitudinal to Latitudinal Formation of Autonomous Aircraft Squadrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17511)
*Paulo André Sperandio Giacomin,Elder Moreira Hemerly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将自主飞行器编队从纵向队形转换为横向队形的算法，基于预定义的3D基本机动动作动态实现，时间复杂度最优，仿真无碰撞且经正确性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编队在执行着陆、起飞、避障和地理参考等任务时，需要同时具备纵向与横向两种队形的能力，因此需要在两种队形之间进行转换。

Method: 利用一组预定义的3D基本机动动作，动态定义队形转换的机动过程，并实现从纵向编队到横向编队的转换算法。

Result: 时间复杂性分析表明该转换算法效率最优；仿真结果显示队形转换过程无碰撞发生。

Conclusion: 正确性证明保证了转换后横向编队的形成特征，验证了该算法在编队转换中的有效性与可靠性。

Abstract: This work presents an algorithm for changing from longitudinal to latitudinal formation of autonomous aircraft squadrons. The maneuvers are defined dynamically by using a predefined set of 3D basic maneuvers. This formation change is necessary when the squadron has to perform tasks demanding both formations, such as landing, lift-off, obstacle avoidance and georeferencing. The time complexity analysis of the transformation algorithm reveals that its efficiency is optimal. Simulations show that the formation change is done without collision, and the proof of correctness ensures its latitudinal formation features.

</details>


### [173] [YUBI-Real2Sim: 3DGS Scene Verification for Reliable Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17521)
*Hiroki Nishizawa,Takehiko Ohkawa,Nicola Scianca,Tatsuya Matsushima,Shigeo Morishima,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 YUBI-Real2Sim 框架，基于 3DGS 实现双臂鱼眼腕部视角下的实景重建与 real-to-sim 策略评估，并构建基准量化重建质量对策略评估的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人上评估 VLA 策略成本高、耗时长且难以复现，真实到仿真（real-to-sim）对规模化评估至关重要。3DGS 虽能快速生成场景，但可靠评估所需的重建质量尚未被量化；尤其依赖腕部鱼眼相机的策略对广角视图中的视觉伪影（空洞、漂浮物）极为敏感，会直接导致任务性能下降。

Method: 构建统一框架 YUBI-Real2Sim，整合 200 度鱼眼相机采集、3DGS 训练、3D 基础模型进行物体建模，以及基于 MuJoCo 的物理仿真；在双臂鱼眼腕部视角下进行场景重建与策略评估。基于该框架搭建覆盖两个双臂机器人平台、三个场景、三个任务、两种 pi0.5 策略的基准，系统测量 real-to-sim 的排序一致性，并针对反事实 3DGS 伪影（天花板空洞、漂浮物、扭曲）和采集扰动评估策略敏感性；同时在场景留出协议下分析新视角质量分数能否指示策略排序可靠性。

Result: 在九组场景-任务对比中有八组复现了真实世界的策略排序；性能表现依任务而异；基于质量分数的可靠性预测存在局限。结果尚未建立通用的场景接受规则。

Conclusion: 使用 Real2Sim 进行策略评估时需评估重建质量，但当前尚无法确立普遍可靠的场景接受准则。

Abstract: Evaluating VLA policies on real robots is costly, time-consuming, and difficult to reproduce, making real-to-sim simulation essential for scalable evaluation. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables rapid real-to-sim scene generation, the reconstruction quality required for reliable policy evaluation remains unquantified. In particular, policies relying on wrist-mounted fisheye cameras (e.g., YUBI handheld gripper data) are heavily exposed to visual artifacts (holes, floaters) in wide-angle views, directly degrading task performance. To address this, we present YUBI-Real2Sim, a unified framework for 3DGS-based scene reconstruction and real-to-sim policy evaluation under dual fisheye wrist views. We develop an integrated pipeline combining 200-degree fisheye camera capture, 3DGS training, 3D foundation models for object modeling, and MuJoCo-based physical simulation, reproducing the observed real-world policy ordering in eight of nine scene-task comparisons. Using this framework, we construct a benchmark across two bimanual robot platforms, three scenes, three tasks, and dual pi0.5-based policies. This systematically measures real-to-sim rank agreement and evaluates policy sensitivity against counterfactual 3DGS artifacts (ceiling holes, floaters, warping) and capture disturbances. We further analyze whether novel-view quality scores can indicate policy-ranking reliability under a scene-held-out evaluation protocol. The results show task-dependent performance and limitations of quality-based reliability prediction, highlighting the need to assess reconstruction quality when using Real2Sim for policy evaluation. These results do not yet establish a generally reliable scene-acceptance rule. Project Page: https://yubi-real2sim.airoa.io.

</details>


### [174] [Box-WAM: Box Trajectories as Compact State Representations for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17526)
*Qinxi Yu,Wenzhou Ding,Gloria X. Wang,Albert J. Zhai,Shenlong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BoxWAM，用任务相关物体的有向包围盒轨迹替代稠密未来视频帧，作为世界动作模型的紧凑未来状态表示，在降低计算成本的同时提升动作预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）通过显式预测动作和未来世界状态，在机器人操作任务中优于仅预测动作的模型。但用全帧视频预测表示未来，在训练和推理阶段计算开销都很大。因此核心问题是：什么样的紧凑状态表示既能保留未来预测带来的规划与监督收益，又能降低计算成本。

Method: 提出BoxWAM，将稠密的未来帧替换为任务相关物体的有向包围盒（oriented bounding box）轨迹。通过显式"想象"以物体为中心的未来运动，提供一个轻量的预测目标来捕捉机器人动作引起的关键物理变化。

Result: 包围盒轨迹预测显著降低了计算成本，同时提升了动作预测性能。

Conclusion: 以物体为中心的紧凑状态表示，可以作为机器人操作中基于视频的世界动作建模的高效替代方案。结果视频见 https://box-wam.github.io。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) are a category of policy models that ex- plicitly predict both actions and future world states. They have been shown to outperform action-only models across various robotic manipulation tasks. How- ever, representing the future with full-frame video prediction can be computation- ally expensive during both training and inference. This raises a central question: what compact state representation preserves the planning and supervision bene- fits of future prediction? In this paper, we propose BoxWAM, which replaces dense future frames with oriented bounding box trajectories of task-relevant ob- jects. By explicitly imagining object-centric future motion, our method provides a lightweight prediction target that captures the key physical changes induced by robot actions. We show that box trajectory prediction substantially reduces com- putational cost while improving action prediction performance, suggesting that object-centric compact states can serve as an efficient alternative to video-based world action modeling for robotic manipulation. Result videos can be found on https://box-wam.github.io

</details>


### [175] [Sim4RAD: A MATLAB Toolkit for Design-Phase Sizing, Actuator Selection and URDF-Model Verification of Serial Robotic Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17534)
*Jingchen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Sim4RAD是一个面向执行器选型与验证的MATLAB工具箱，可从少量参数构建刚体模型并执行静力学、动力学、随机运动及工作空间/灵巧度四类分析；以7自由度拟人臂为例，比较了三种布局，表明肩关节轴序影响很小，而腕部末端关节与负载同轴可大幅降低力矩但牺牲灵巧度，且目标速度场景对力矩需求的影响远大于轨迹本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行器选型发生在设计早期且影响深远：关节力矩与速度规格会在任何CAD模型出现之前就确定电机、减速器和制动器。因此需要一个能在该阶段生成规格、并在后续设计阶段用于验证的工具。

Method: Sim4RAD用少量参数构建刚体模型，通过四类分析给出规格：(1) 静力学分析结合力臂采样与姿态搜索，在给定负载包络下给出额定力矩和可复现的峰值力矩；(2) 动力学流程在四种约束（含执行器力矩-速度包络）之一下重塑轨迹；(3) 对大量随机运动进行随机分析，使规格基于一类运动而非单一轨迹；(4) 工作空间与灵巧度分析，区分可达范围与可达方式。将合成模型替换为URDF文件后，同一套分析可用于验证。

Result: 以7自由度拟人臂在三种布局（肩轴顺序不同、末端腕关节是否与负载同轴）下对比：半臂长处的静力需求三者几乎相同，且由关节而非采样运动决定规格；重排肩轴顺序使最坏力矩保持55 N·m不变，可达体积差异在8%以内。末端腕关节与负载同轴可消除其重力力矩，使相邻腕关节降低近三分之一，并在5 m/s扫描中使末端关节需求降低十倍，但灵巧度下降（均值0.570降至0.348）。同一手臂在5 m/s目标下所需力矩是2 s运动的20至37倍。

Conclusion: 需先确定运动场景而非轨迹，因为场景对力矩需求的影响远大于轨迹。验证应使用详细设计的URDF模型。工具箱已开源于https://github.com/LasterLi/Sim4RAD。

Abstract: Actuator sizing is an early and consequential decision: joint torque and velocity specifications fix the motors, gearboxes and brakes before any CAD model exists. Sim4RAD is a MATLAB toolkit for that phase and for the verification that follows it. It builds a rigid-body model from a handful of parameters, and four analyses supply the specification: a static analysis combining lever-arm sampling with a posture search, giving a rated torque at a chosen payload envelope and a reproducible peak torque; a dynamics pipeline that reshapes a trajectory under one of four constraints, including the actuator torque-speed envelope; a stochastic analysis over many random motions, so a specification rests on a class of motions, not one trajectory; and a workspace and dexterity analysis that separates where the arm can reach from how it can reach there. Replacing the synthesized model by a URDF file turns the same analyses to verification. A 7-DOF anthropomorphic arm is compared in three layouts differing in the shoulder axis order and in whether the last wrist joint is coaxial with the payload. The static demand at half arm length is nearly the same in all three, and it sizes the joints rather than the sampled motion; reordering the shoulder axes leaves the worst-case torque unchanged at 55 N m and the reachable volume within 8 percent. A last wrist joint coaxial with the payload removes its gravity torque, reduces the adjacent wrist joint by almost a third and cuts the last joint demand tenfold in a 5 m/s scan, at the price of dexterity (mean 0.570 to 0.348). The same arm needs 20 to 37 times more torque for a 5 m/s target than for a 2 s motion: the scenario, not the trajectory, must be fixed first. Verification should use the URDF model of the detailed design; the toolkit is available at https://github.com/LasterLi/Sim4RAD.

</details>


### [176] [ATOM: Abstract Transition and Option Modeling - Experience-Informed Hierarchical Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17555)
*Changxuan Yao,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ATOM：结合抽象图规划与可复用option策略的分层导航框架，在大规模3D环境中显著降低重复规划时间并保持近最优轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模3D环境中重复导航任务效率低，单体MDP规划计算代价高，需要利用分层结构与可复用策略来加速规划。
