This design preserves accurate trajectory evaluation while reducing redundant rollout computation. Experiments on RECON, HuRoN, SCAND, and TartanDrive show that, compared with NWM, SkipWorld reduces the average ATE by 4.2% and RPE by 2.7%, while decreasing planning time by 62.4% with a 2.7-fold speedup.

</details>


### [212] [Walk Like This: Stylized Locomotion for Physics-Based Characters through Example-Driven Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18147)
*Joshua Aurand,Agon Serifi,Sammy Christen,Ruben Grandia,Espen Knoop,Marco Hutter,Moritz Bächer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出实时表达性腿部运动框架，实现速度与风格的独立控制，利用大规模非结构化运动数据并泛化到未见风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动控制器通常需要显式风格标签进行训练，难以利用非结构化数据且难以泛化到未见风格。

Method: 通过定制采样策略解耦风格与功能性运动，训练风格编码器；其输出条件化自回归扩散模型。推理时输入风格参考片段与摇杆速度命令，扩散模型生成运动学参考，由强化学习控制器跟踪；并引入注意力偏置机制与速度命令校正策略。

Result: 在仿真物理人形角色与真实机器人上验证，实现鲁棒的实时控制与连续风格调制。

Conclusion: 该方法无需显式风格标签，可高效利用非结构化数据并泛化到未见风格，实现速度与风格的独立、连续可控。

Abstract: We present a real-time framework for expressive legged locomotion that enables independent control over velocity and style. Unlike existing data-driven controllers, which typically require explicit style labels for training, our method efficiently leverages large-scale unstructured motion data and generalizes to unseen styles. We disentangle style from functional locomotion through a tailored sampling strategy used to train a style encoder whose output conditions an autoregressive diffusion model. During inference, the user provides a style reference clip and a joystick velocity command. The diffusion model then generates a kinematic reference tracked by a reinforcement learning controller. To improve user controllability and stylization strength, we propose an attention biasing mechanism and a velocity command correction strategy. We demonstrate our method on a physics-based humanoid character in simulation and on a physical robot, showcasing robust real-time control and continuous style modulation. The results can be best seen in our supplemental video (https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=roFnS6N7nJ4).

</details>


### [213] [Force Sensor-Based Picking Pattern Analysis and Two-Jaw Gripper Design for Cherry Tomato Robotic Harvesting in Greenhouse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18160)
*Rajmeet Singh,Manveen Kaur,Yusuf Adamu,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于力数据驱动的自主樱桃番茄采摘框架，集成六轴力/力矩传感、仿生柔性夹爪与移动机器人平台，在商业温室中实现了86.0%的采摘成功率、2.3%的损伤率与8.4秒/果的作业周期，验证了握持-扭转-拉拽混合策略的初步可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温室樱桃番茄采摘面临果实簇密集、果皮娇嫩、需轻柔且有效分离等挑战。传统末端执行器（真空、旋转、夹持、吸切等）在成功率与损伤控制上表现不佳，因此需要一种结合人类采摘经验与力感知的自主采摘方案。

Method: 研究采用ATI六轴力/力矩传感器安装于食指，采集150次人工采摘试验（握持、扭转、侧向拉拽三种基本动作各50次）的力学特征。据此设计指尖局部柔性Finray夹爪（接触区为TPU 95A柔性几何、主体为刚性PLA），模拟人类指尖双刚度结构；采用Dynamixel XM430-W350伺服电机进行基于电流的力矩控制，施加测定握持力与45°腕部扭转。通过有限元分析验证应力水平，并集成于UR5机械臂与Clearpath Husky A200移动平台，在NVIDIA Isaac Sim仿真及阿布扎比SILAL商业温室开展50次自主试验。

Result: 握持需峰值法向力3.92 N与0.45 N；扭转在果柄轴上产生主导力矩19.20±2.10 Nmm且对果簇扰动最小；单纯侧向拉拽不足以可靠分离。有限元分析显示3.92 N载荷下最大von Mises应力为0.24 MPa，低于樱桃番茄果皮损伤阈值。混合握持-扭转-拉拽策略采摘成功率达86.0%，果实损伤率2.3%，单果周期8.4秒，优于真空（66.3%）、旋转（70.1%）、夹持（76.0%）与吸切（52.4%）末端执行器。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在受试设施与品种条件下，该力数据驱动的仿生采摘框架具有初步可行性，其混合策略在成功率、损伤控制与作业效率方面均优于对比末端执行器，为温室樱桃番茄自动化采摘提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Robotic harvesting of cherry tomatoes in greenhouses remains challenging due to dense fruit clusters, delicate skin, and the need for gentle yet effective detachment. This study presents a force-data-driven framework for autonomous cherry tomato harvesting that integrates six-axis force/torque sensing, a bio-inspired compliant gripper, and deployment on a mobile robotic platform. A wearable ATI six-axis force/torque sensor mounted on the index finger captured kinetic signatures of three elemental picking patterns (gripping, twisting, and lateral pulling) across 150 manual trials (50 per pattern) in laboratory and greenhouse settings. Gripping required a peak normal force of 3.92 and 0.45 N, twisting produced a dominant torque of 19.20 2.10 Nmm about the pedicel axis with minimal truss disturbance, and pure lateral pulling alone was insufficient for reliable detachment. These measurements informed a novel fingertip-localised Finray gripper, with compliant TPU 95A Finray geometry restricted to the fruit-contact region and a rigid PLA body, emulating the dual-stiffness human fingertip. A Dynamixel XM430-W350 servomotor under current-based torque control applies the measured gripping force and a 45° wrist twist. Finite element analysis showed a maximum von Mises stress of 0.24 MPa under the 3.92 N load, below the bruising threshold of cherry tomato skin. Integrated on a UR5 arm and Clearpath Husky A200 base, the system was validated in NVIDIA Isaac Sim and in 50 autonomous trials at the SILAL commercial greenhouse, Abu Dhabi, UAE. The hybrid grip-twist-pull strategy achieved an 86.0% picking success rate, 2.3% fruit damage, and an 8.4 s cycle time per fruit, outperforming vacuum (66.3%), rotating (70.1%), clamping (76.0%), and suction-cutting (52.4%) end-effectors. These results demonstrate preliminary feasibility under the tested facility and cultivar conditions

</details>


### [214] [RESTA: Time-Scaling Augmentation for Data-Efficient Robot Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18163)
*Yunpeng Jiang,Jianshu Hu,Han Fang,Zhiyuan Guan,Paul Weng,Yutong Ban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RESTA（逆转与步幅轨迹增强），通过轨迹逆转和时间重采样两种时间尺度数据增强提升机器人模仿学习的数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习通常依赖昂贵演示数据，且演示往往只在一个任务方向和单一时间分辨率下采集，数据效率低下。作者希望在不增加环境交互的前提下，通过时间尺度增强复用已有演示来提升数据效率。

Method: RESTA包含两部分：1）轨迹逆转增强——对具有相反目标的成对任务之间进行逆转并重打标签，复用已有rollout而不需额外环境交互；由于接触丰富的操作并非严格可逆，将逆转视为近似的成对任务先验而非物理对称。2）时间步幅增强——改变观测与动作的采样步幅，并将产生的偏移作为策略条件，支持固定或随机的时间调度。

Result: 在16个成对双臂操作任务上实验：轨迹逆转在低数据场景下尤为有效，时间步幅增强提升了随机时间调度下的成功率。每任务仅用25和50条独立演示时，RESTA分别达到78.4%和85.2%的平均成功率，匹配或超过使用两倍演示（77.3%和85.4%）训练的基线。

Conclusion: RESTA通过逆转与步幅的时间尺度增强，在无需额外环境交互的情况下显著提升了机器人模仿学习的数据效率，为降低演示成本提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies trained through imitation learning commonly rely on costly demonstrations recorded in one task direction and at one temporal resolution. We propose Reversal and Stride-based Trajectory Augmentation (RESTA), which exploits time-scaling data augmentation to improve data efficiency through trajectory reversal and temporal resampling. Trajectory-reversal augmentation reverses and relabels demonstrations between paired tasks with opposite objectives, reusing recorded rollouts without additional environment interaction. Because contact-rich manipulation is not exactly reversible, we treat reversal as an approximate paired-task prior rather than a physical symmetry. Temporal-stride augmentation varies observation and action sampling strides and conditions the policy on the resulting offsets, enabling fixed or randomized temporal schedules. Experiments across 16 paired bimanual manipulation tasks show that trajectory reversal is particularly effective in the low-data regime and that temporal-stride augmentation improves success under randomized temporal schedules. With 25 and 50 independently collected demonstrations per task, RESTA achieves 78.4% and 85.2% average success, respectively, matching or exceeding baselines trained with twice as many demonstrations (77.3% and 85.4%). These results show that RESTA can improve data efficiency in robot imitation learning.

</details>


### [215] [Beyond Planning: Differentiable Control-in-the-loop End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18167)
*Zhidong Wang,Zirui Li,Zitong Shan,Zhiqi Mao,Qihang Huang,Zhan Chen,Jingsong Liang,Han Yu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DIVA将可辨识的执行器级车辆模型嵌入端到端自动驾驶网络训练回路，形成从感知到车辆状态的可微链路，用学习型控制解码器替代外置跟踪控制器，解决不可微动力学、标签混叠、速率不匹配及模型失配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶虽能直接从原始传感器数据规划轨迹，但规划到控制仍需人工调参的跟踪控制器，切断了从车辆运动回到网络的梯度路径。这一鸿沟源于车辆动力学不可微、粗采样专家标签造成的监督混叠、高频率控制与低频率重规划的速率不匹配，以及训练与部署间的车辆模型差异。

Method: 提出DIVA：将辨识得到的执行器级车辆模型嵌入端到端驾驶网络训练回路，构建从感知到车辆状态的可微链路；用基于学习的控制解码器替代外部控制器，在滚出（rolled-out）车辆状态上监督，绕开混叠的控制输入标签；采用速率一致的控制输入参数化以协调异构采样率，实现3.8ms纯控制周期的异步执行；并通过在线策略重辨识与在线扰动观测器分别应对训练时分布偏移和部署时模型失配。

Result: 在闭环Bench2Drive基准上，DIVA取得86.7的驾驶得分，比广泛采用的最先进方法高出3.3分，同时平均jerk几乎减半、刹车切换减少64%，并在部署时存在显著车辆模型失配的情况下仍保持优异的路径跟踪精度。

Conclusion: 通过在训练回路中嵌入可微的辨识车辆动力学并采用学习型控制解码器，DIVA打通了端到端自动驾驶从规划到控制的梯度通路，兼顾了控制平顺性、跟踪精度与部署鲁棒性。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has achieved remarkable progress by planning trajectories directly from raw sensor data. Yet the step from planning to control still relies on hand-tuned tracking controllers that block the gradient path from vehicle motion back to the network. This gap stems from non-differentiable vehicle dynamics, aliased supervision from coarsely sampled expert labels, rate mismatch between high-rate control and low-rate re-planning, and vehicle model discrepancies between training and deployment. This paper proposes DIVA (Differentiable Identified Vehicle dynamics for end-to-end Autonomous driving) to close this gap. An identified actuator-level vehicle model is embedded in the training loop of the end-to-end driving network, forming a differentiable chain from perception to vehicle states. A learning-based control decoder replaces the external controller and is supervised on the rolled-out vehicle states, bypassing the aliased control-input labels. A rate-consistent control-input parameterization reconciles the heterogeneous sampling rates and enables asynchronous execution with a 3.8 ms control-only cycle. On-policy re-identification and an online disturbance observer address training-time distribution shift and deployment-time model mismatch respectively. On the closed-loop Bench2Drive benchmark, DIVA achieves a driving score of 86.7, surpassing the widely adopted state-of-the-art approach by 3.3 points while nearly halving mean jerk and reducing brake switching by 64%, and maintains superior path tracking accuracy under significant vehicle model mismatch at deployment.

</details>


### [216] [OneDemo: Unified Policy Learning and Data Generation from a Single Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18188)
*Ying Qian,Jianshu Hu,Han Fang,Shuguang Li,Yutong Ban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OneDemo 提出一个强化学习框架，通过"编辑"单个成功演示来生成多样化成功轨迹，并同时作为任务策略，在 Meta-World 与 DexMimicGen 任务上取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从演示学习机器人策略常受限于采集多样、高质量专家数据的成本。将单个成功演示扩展为多样化成功轨迹是有吸引力的替代方案，但现有增强方法依赖手工设计的变换规则或复杂轨迹规划，需要大量任务特定工程与人力。

Method: 将演示编辑建模为结构化强化学习问题：编辑器从稀疏的任务成功奖励中学习两件事——何时在面向对象的轨迹片段之间进行切换，以及如何对每个片段进行几何自适应。学到的编辑器既可作为任务策略，也可作为可复用的演示生成器。

Result: 在 15 个 Meta-World 任务上平均 53.7k 次环境交互内达到 92.7% 成功率；在 9 个 DexMimicGen 任务上生成的成功演示平均成功率 98.4%，在匹配数据集规模下用生成数据训练的 BC-RNN 策略平均成功率达 99.6%。

Conclusion: 学习编辑单个成功演示为策略学习和可扩展的演示生成提供了结构化且有效的基础。

Abstract: Learning robot policies from demonstrations is often limited by the cost of collecting diverse, high-quality expert data. While an appealing alternative is to expand a single successful demonstration into a diverse set of successful trajectories, existing augmentation methods typically rely on manually designed transformation rules or complex trajectory planning, requiring substantial task-specific engineering and human effort. We introduce OneDemo, a reinforcement learning framework that learns to edit a single successful demonstration for new task configurations. We formulate demonstration editing as a structured reinforcement learning problem, in which an editor learns, from sparse task-success rewards, both when to transition between object-centric trajectory segments and how to geometrically adapt each segment. The learned editor serves both as a task policy and as a reusable demonstration generator. Across 15 Meta-World tasks, OneDemo reaches a 92.7% success rate within 53.7k environment interactions on average. Across 9 DexMimicGen tasks, it generates successful demonstrations with a 98.4% average success rate, and BC-RNN policies trained on the generated data achieve 99.6% average success at matched dataset scale. These results show that learning to edit a single successful demonstration provides a structured and effective basis for both policy learning and scalable demonstration generation.

</details>


### [217] [Cross-Embodiment Affordance Transfer for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18194)
*Sawera Yaseen,Serge Thill,Rekha Raja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个将人类可供性标注转换为机器人可执行可供性预测的框架，以机器人几何与约束为条件，在仿真中结合人类标注与执行结果训练，实验证明可供性预测必须以机器人为条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作系统的可供性学习多依赖人类标注数据集，这类数据反映的是人手可交互的区域，而非特定机器人的物理约束。人类标注为可抓取的表面区域，对机器人而言可能几何上不可达或机械上不可行，而这种不匹配此前很少被显式处理。

Method: 提出一个以机器人物理属性为条件、将人类可供性标注转化为机器人可执行可供性预测的框架。网络输入为物体三维点云与编码机器人几何和约束的描述符，输出物体表面哪些区域对该机器人物理上可执行。训练数据由人类标注可供性与仿真中多次采样接近试验的机器人执行结果共同构成，执行结果决定哪些区域对机器人可执行。

Result: 在家庭物品数据集上、使用UR5e机械臂的两种平行夹爪配置进行评估，该框架优于未接收机器人信息的对照模型。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，可供性预测必须以机器人为条件，仅凭人类标注不足以学习到机器人可执行的可供性表示。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation systems commonly learn object affordances from human-annotated datasets, which reflect interaction possibilities for a human hand rather than the physical constraints of a specific robot. For example, a surface region of an object identified as graspable by a human annotator may be geometrically unreachable or mechanically infeasible for a robot, yet this mismatch is rarely addressed explicitly. To tackle this challenge, we proposed a framework that translates human affordance annotations into robot-executable affordance predictions conditioned on the physical properties of the robot. The network predicts which surface regions of an object are physically executable by the robot, given a 3D point cloud of an object and a descriptor encoding the robot's geometry and constraints. The model was trained using human-annotated affordances together with robotic execution outcomes from multiple sampled approach trials in simulation, where execution outcomes determined which regions are executable for the robot. The evaluation was performed on a household objects dataset using two parallel-jaw gripper configurations on a UR5e robot arm. The framework outperformed a model that does not receive information about the robot. The experimental results demonstrated that affordance prediction must be conditioned on the robot and that human annotations alone are insufficient for learning robot-executable affordance representations.

</details>


### [218] [I2O2I-Nav: A Benchmark with a Modular Agent for Long-Horizon Indoor-to-Outdoor-to-Indoor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18307)
*Zhengjie Shu,Wanyu Li,Di Xiao,Lingxin Jiang,Lai Jiang,Kaidi Hu,Xincheng Tang,Wenkang Hu,Haoran Wang,Jinchuan Zhang,Chang Chen,Wei Li,Jia Pan,Ruigang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出物理仿真基准 I2O2I-Nav 与 MODULAR-Agent，评估室内外连续长时程导航的组合能力，揭示完整任务成功率极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务与配送机器人需完成跨越室内外的长时程导航任务，而现有具身导航基准多只评估孤立阶段或部分跨域行程，难以衡量异构导航能力在连续执行下的组合表现。

Method: 构建 I2O2I-Nav 基准：20 个真实复合场景、超 115,000 平方米、多建筑多楼层，含对齐的 3DGS 渲染与碰撞几何；设三级评估（独立原子能力→两阶段组合→完整四阶段 I2O2I），每阶段以上一阶段真实终点为起点，并用阶段条件指标追踪完成度与失败定位。同时提出 MODULAR-Agent，通过完成验证、阶段交接与有界恢复来协调冻结的导航策略。

Result: 在 1,374 个回合上评估，凸显连续组合的难度：MODULAR-Agent 在单个原子能力上成功率 40.5%–76%，但完整 I2O2I 成功率仍很低；相比使用相同底层策略的固定策略链，其在 I2O 与 O2I 上成功率提升一倍以上，完整任务成功率从 1.15% 提升至 4.02%。阶段分析显示其能让更多任务到达最终物体导航阶段，而该阶段条件成功率仍偏低。

Conclusion: 连续长时程导航的组合执行仍是重大挑战，难点既在于长时程阶段执行，也在于完成判定；模块化协调机制优于固定策略链，但完整任务性能仍有很大提升空间。

Abstract: Service and delivery robots often need to complete long-horizon navigation missions spanning indoor and outdoor environments. Existing embodied navigation benchmarks primarily evaluate isolated stages or partial cross-domain journeys, providing limited support for assessing how heterogeneous navigation capabilities compose under continuous execution. We introduce I2O2I-Nav, a physics-enabled benchmark of 20 real-world composite scenes spanning over 115,000 square meters across multiple buildings and floors, with aligned 3DGS rendering and collision geometry. Its three-level evaluation progresses from independent primitives to two-stage compositions and complete four-stage I2O2I missions. Each stage starts from its predecessor's actual endpoint, while stage-conditional metrics track completion and localize failures along the mission. We further propose the MODULAR-Agent that coordinates frozen navigation policies through completion verification, stage handoffs, and bounded recovery. Evaluation across 1,374 episodes highlights the difficulty of continuous composition. The MODULAR-Agent achieves 40.5-76% success on individual primitives, but full I2O2I success remains low. Compared with a fixed policy chain using the same underlying policies, it more than doubles success on both Indoor-to-Outdoor (I2O) and Outdoor-to-Indoor (O2I), while improving full-mission success from 1.15% to 4.02%. Stage-wise analysis shows that the MODULAR-Agent enables more missions to reach the final object-navigation stage, where conditional success remains low, revealing challenges in both long-horizon stage execution and completion decisions.

</details>


### [219] [CoSMO: Conformal Semantic Mapping for Open-World Safe Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18321)
*Shuqi Wang,Yiwei Zheng,Pingrui Zhang,Yue Gao,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoSMO框架，将VLM嵌入转化为具有覆盖保证的校准语义安全集，实现开放世界下的安全机器人导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全自主导航不仅需要几何避障，还需语义感知：不同物体类别需要不同安全间距，且相关类别集合会随探索扩展。VLM虽提供丰富语义表示，但其置信度校准差，原始预测不可靠；现有共形预测方法假设标签集固定且数据平稳，难以用于开放世界场景。

Method: CoSMO在动态扩展的危险词汇表上，将VLM嵌入转化为校准的覆盖集与安全感知的障碍膨胀，并在分布偏移下保持有效性，即便面对延迟的专家反馈。

Result: 在Matterport3D数据集上，相比仅几何基线，CoSMO将语义碰撞减少61.6%，并在延迟专家反馈下仍保持形式化覆盖保证；四足机器人真实实验中实现90.73%的长期经验覆盖率（目标90%）。

Conclusion: CoSMO通过共形语义建图实现了开放世界下的安全机器人导航，兼具覆盖保证与跨环境泛化能力。

Abstract: Safe autonomous navigation requires not only geometric obstacle avoidance but also semantic awareness. Different object categories demand different safety clearances, and the set of relevant categories may expand as the robot explores novel environments. Vision-language models (VLMs) offer rich semantic representations for building maps of unknown scenes, yet their confidence scores are poorly calibrated, making raw predictions unreliable for safety-critical planning. Conformal prediction (CP) can provide coverage guarantees, but existing methods assume a fixed label set and stationary data, limiting their use in open-world settings. We propose Conformal Semantic Mapping for Open-World Safe Robot Navigation (CoSMO), a semantic safety mapping framework that transforms VLM embeddings into calibrated coverage sets and safety-aware obstacle dilations over a dynamically expanding hazard vocabulary, even under distribution shift. Experiments on the Matterport3D (MP3D) dataset show that CoSMO reduces semantic collisions by 61.6% relative to a geometry-only baseline, while maintaining formal coverage guarantees even under delayed expert feedback. Real-world experiments on a quadruped robot further demonstrate generalization by achieving long-run empirical coverage of 90.73% against the 90% target. Project website: https://yangming911.github.io/cosmo-page/.

</details>


### [220] [Let the Mind Conceive, Let the Hand Achieve: Following Multimodal Instructions for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18352)
*Youpeng Wen,Gang Wang,Tianyu Huang,Weiwei Zhang,Zhijian Huang,Wei Chen,Xieyuanli Chen,Yunhui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Orchestra 是一种将任务规划与执行管理从动作生成中分离的智能体框架，使机器人能够在场景变化中按正确顺序操作正确物体，并在长序列子任务中保持进度感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人执行多步指令时，需要在场景变化中按正确顺序作用于正确物体，并持续追踪任务进度、判断何时推进到下一步。而已有方法多基于原子指令和短轨迹训练，难以胜任更长的子任务序列。

Method: Orchestra 将完整多模态指令分解为有序子任务，跟踪已完成步骤，并在执行过程中更新相关物体与目标位置。它维护一个共享上下文，包含当前活跃子任务、空间 grounding 和近期观测；动作策略基于该上下文生成动作，另有一个单独训练的进度评估器判断何时进入下一子任务。

Result: 在九个 RMBench 任务上，Orchestra 平均成功率达 68.22%，比 Mem-0 高 26.22 个百分点；在新提出的 RMBench-Compose 基准上，平均成功长度（ASL）为 7.35 个子任务，是 HarnessVLA 的 2.46 倍；在四个真实世界多步任务上，相比使用基础 π0.5 策略的 Orchestra，平均 ASL 提升 25.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，Orchestra 能帮助机器人执行更长的子任务序列，同时保持所要求的子任务顺序并作用于指定物体，验证了将规划与执行管理同动作生成解耦的有效性。

Abstract: A robot following a multi-step instruction needs to act on the right objects in the right order, even as the scene changes. It must also maintain awareness of task progress and determine when to advance. To address these challenges, we introduce Orchestra, an agent harness that separates task planning and execution management from action generation. Orchestra decomposes a complete multimodal instruction into ordered subtasks, tracks completed steps, and updates the locations of relevant objects and destinations during execution. It maintains a shared context containing the active subtask, spatial grounding, and recent observations. An action policy uses this context to generate actions, while a separately trained progress evaluator estimates when to advance to the next subtask. To evaluate whether robots trained on atomic instructions and short trajectories can execute longer subtask sequences, we introduce a benchmark named RMBench-Compose. Orchestra achieves a mean success rate of $68.22\%$ across nine RMBench tasks, outperforming Mem-0 by $26.22$ percentage points. On RMBench-Compose, it achieves an Average Success Length (ASL) of $7.35$ subtasks, $2.46$ times that of HarnessVLA. Across four real-world multi-step tasks, it improves mean ASL by $25.0\%$ over Orchestra using the base $π_{0.5}$ policy. These results show that Orchestra helps robots carry out longer sequences of subtasks while preserving the requested subtask order and acting on the specified objects. Our code and project page are available at https://youpengwen.github.io/Orchestra.

</details>


### [221] [Visual Sevoing from Lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18363)
*Nicolas Andreff,Bernard Espiau,Radu Horaud*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于直线的新型视觉伺服方法，通过引入"双归一化Plücker坐标"表示法，实现图像直线的正确对齐，并给出了具有全局渐近稳定性的控制律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉伺服多基于点特征，直线特征具有更强的结构表达能力，但直线表示方式繁多且不易直接用于伺服控制，需要研究适合视觉伺服的直线表示法及对应的对齐概念与控制律。

Method: 对主流直线表示法进行理论与几何研究，提出新的"双归一化Plücker坐标"表示法；基于该表示法定义图像直线对齐概念，并设计控制律，分析其旋转与平移的部分解耦、运动方程的解析求逆及全局渐近稳定条件。

Result: 所设计控制律在仿真和实验中得到验证，实验场景为特定情况下的正交三面体（orthogonal trihedron）。

Conclusion: 双归一化Plücker坐标适合视觉伺服，能够支持合理的图像直线对齐，并带来部分解耦、解析求逆与全局渐近稳定等良好性质，方法在仿真与实验中均有效。

Abstract: In this work, we present a fundamentally new approach to visual servoing using lines. It is based on a theoretical and geometrical study of the main line representations which allowed us to define a new representation, the so-called binormalized Plucker coordinates. They are particularly well suited to visual servoing. Indeed, they allow the definition of a proper image line alignment concept. The control law which realizes such an alignment has moreover several properties: partial decoupling between rotation and translation, analytical inversion of the motion equations and global asymptotic stability conditions. This control law was validated both in simulation and experimentally in the specific case of an orthogonal trihedron.

</details>


### [222] [DR-VLA: Learning Human-Like Quadruped Navigation from Egocentric Web Videos via Domain-Invariant Scene Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18373)
*Jun Tian,Yingjie Niu,Ruo Chen,Hongyi Lin,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DR-VLA 通过域不变场景表示将第一视角网络视频中的人类导航经验迁移到四足机器人，在导航评分上提升约5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在户外配送中需理解复杂场景并具备社交合规导航能力，但机器人示范数据采集成本高、难以规模化；网络第一视角视频虽丰富，却因视角和运动差异难以直接迁移给机器人。

Method: 提出 DR-VLA（Mixture-of-Transformers 框架），借助域不变场景表示（DISR）迁移人类导航经验，结合视觉语言理解、潜在世界建模与流匹配动作专家；构建 EgoLynx 数据集（1万小时网络视频 EgoLynx-Web 与2千段遥操作机器人导航 EgoLynx-Robot）；先在无动作标签的网络视频上预训练理解与世界专家，再适配机器人观测并训练动作专家；提出四足导航评分 QNS。

Result: 在留出的 EgoLynx-Robot 测试集上，QNS 相对最优对比结果提升约5%，开环最终航向误差（FHE）降低14.6%；消融显示网络视频预训练提升泛化能力。作为未来预测目标，DISR 优于五种通用视觉表示，接近任务专用3D感知。在 NAVSIM v1 上单独训练（无网络预训练）达到92.1 PDMS。

Conclusion: 通过域不变场景表示迁移网络视频中的人类导航经验，可有效提升四足机器人导航性能，且该框架有望推广至自动驾驶等领域。

Abstract: Quadruped robots performing outdoor deliveries must interpret complex scenes, anticipate changes, and navigate in socially compliant ways. Robot demonstrations are costly to collect at the scale needed for generalization. Egocentric web videos offer abundant human navigation experience, but differences in viewpoint and motion make this experience difficult to transfer to robots. We introduce DR-VLA, a Mixture-of-Transformers framework that transfers human navigation experience through a domain-invariant scene representation (DISR). The framework combines vision-language understanding and latent world modeling with a flow-matching action expert. We introduce EgoLynx, comprising 10,000 hours of curated egocentric web video (EgoLynx-Web) and 2,000 clips of teleoperated robot navigation collected during real-world deliveries (EgoLynx-Robot). We pretrain the understanding and world experts on EgoLynx-Web without action labels, then adapt the model to robot observations and train the action expert on EgoLynx-Robot to predict executable trajectories. We also propose the Quadruped Navigation Score (QNS), a pseudo-closed-loop metric combining safety, instruction compliance, comfort, and progress. On the held-out EgoLynx-Robot test split, DR-VLA improves QNS by approximately 5% and reduces open-loop final heading error (FHE) by 14.6%, each relative to the best competing result for that metric. Ablations show that web-video pretraining improves generalization and that the model's key components contribute to performance. As a future-prediction target, DISR outperforms five generic visual representations and yields performance close to task-specific 3D perception without dedicated geometric labels or point clouds for target construction. Trained separately on NAVSIM v1 without web pretraining, DR-VLA achieves 92.1 PDMS, suggesting that the framework also applies to autonomous driving.

</details>


### [223] [Benchmarking Interaction-Oriented Aerial Mobility across Heterogeneous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18381)
*Zhibo Duan,Hanlin Kong,Tianran Ma,Yuze Wu,Yuxuan Xu,Kenghou Hoi,Changheng Xie,Xin Zhou,Jianzhe Gao,Fei Gao,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CrossFly是面向异构环境长时程交互式空中移动的基准，含15K轨迹、20+重建场景，并提出交互就绪率IRR指标，揭示室内外转换场景比单一环境更难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中基准多局限于单一环境，难以评估跨连续环境转换的智能体；且在评估交互时，智能体常被初始化在目标附近，导致从导航到交互的过渡过程缺乏评估。

Method: 提出CrossFly基准，在统一仿真与评估框架下涵盖20多个重建场景的15K条轨迹，分为室内、室外及室内↔室外三类；提出交互就绪率（IRR）评估终端导航状态是否为下游交互提供合适条件，无需假设具体操作策略；并在五个代表性空中VLA智能体与提出的CrossFly-Agent上进行实验。

Result: 室内↔室外场景比单一环境更具挑战性，在未见场景上最高成功率仅9.7%；下游操作案例研究进一步验证了面向交互的到达对后续物理交互的相关性。

Conclusion: CrossFly有望推动空中具身智能向异构环境中统一移动与物理交互发展。

Abstract: Aerial mobile manipulation requires unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to traverse heterogeneous environments such as indoor and outdoor domains, and connect navigation with subsequent physical interaction within the same trajectories. Existing aerial benchmarks are typically confined to single-environment settings, limiting evaluation of agents across continuous environmental transitions. When interaction is evaluated, agents are often initialized near the target, leaving the transition from navigation to interaction largely unassessed. This work introduces CrossFly, a benchmark for long-horizon interaction-oriented aerial mobility across heterogeneous environments. CrossFly contains 15K trajectories across more than 20 reconstructed scenes under a unified simulation and evaluation framework, covering Indoor, Outdoor, and Indoor$\leftrightarrow$Outdoor trajectories. It further introduces Interaction Readiness Rate (IRR) to assess whether terminal navigation states provide suitable conditions for downstream interaction without assuming a specific manipulation policy. Experiments with five representative aerial vision-language-action (VLA) agents and the proposed CrossFly-Agent show that Indoor$\leftrightarrow$Outdoor settings are more challenging than single-environment settings, with the highest success rate reaching 9.7% on unseen scenes. A downstream manipulation case study further validates the relevance of interaction-oriented arrival to subsequent physical interaction. We hope CrossFly helps advance aerial embodied intelligence toward unified mobility and physical interaction in heterogeneous environments.

</details>


### [224] [Intent-guided Adaptive Stiffening for Visuomotor Policies under Unknown Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18386)
*Francesco Cufino,Carmela Mariniello,Mario Selvaggio,Andreas Sochopoulos,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个残差增刚层，置于未修改策略之下，无需力传感、无需重训练即可应对高摩擦阻力操作任务，成功率从52%提升至90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拧瓶盖、沿表面滑动零件等操作任务需要沿高摩擦方向施加力，机器人必须克服阻力而非顺从阻力。生成式视觉运动策略只预测运动却不预测实现运动所需的"权限"，而该权限受限于其下方的阻抗控制器：当阻力超过柔性控制器在有限跟踪误差内所能施加的力时，无论预测多准确任务都无法完成。所需力的大小还依赖于观测无法揭示的接触状态，因此无法预先估计。

Method: 在未修改的策略之下放置一个残差增刚层。无需力传感，通过策略指令运动与机器人实际运动之间的不匹配来识别阻力；一个四模式监督器沿单一方向提高刚度，该方向从策略自身的动作块中读取，在任务推进期间保持刚度，任务结束后释放。该层无需柔顺标签、无需重训练、无需重新采集演示数据。

Result: 在两个真实任务（拧瓶盖、在非均匀摩擦下沿T型槽推动T型螺母）上，在六个阻力水平下评估（其中两个水平未出现在演示中），该层将成功率从52%提升至90%，同时相比同等阻力下的统一刚度控制器，峰值轴向外力减少了61%。

Conclusion: 通过从指令运动与实际运动的偏差中识别阻力，并沿策略动作隐含的方向动态提高刚度，可在不修改策略、不依赖力传感的情况下显著提升高摩擦阻力操作任务的成功率并降低轴向外力。

Abstract: Manipulation tasks such as unscrewing a cap or sliding a part in contact with a surface require imposing force along a high-friction direction: the robot must overcome a resistance rather than yield to it. Generative visuomotor policies predict the motion for such tasks but not the authority needed to realize it, and that authority is bounded by the impedance controller beneath them: when the resistance exceeds what a compliant controller can exert under a bounded tracking error, the task is unattainable however accurate the prediction is. The required amount cannot be anticipated either, since it depends on a contact state the observation does not reveal. In this paper, we propose a residual stiffening layer placed underneath an unmodified policy. Resistance is recognized, without force sensing, from the mismatch between the motion the policy commands and the motion the robot realizes, and a four-mode supervisor raises the stiffness along a single direction read off the policy's own action chunk, retains it while the task progresses and releases it afterwards. The layer needs no compliance labels, no retraining and no re-collected demonstrations. On two real tasks, unscrewing a bottle cap and pushing a T-nut along a T-slot groove against non-homogeneous friction, evaluated at six resistance levels of which two were withheld from the demonstrations, the layer raises the success rate from 52% to 90% while producing 61% less peak off-axis force than a uniformly stiff controller at the same resistance.

</details>


### [225] [CALM: Decentralized Control of Cooperative Aerial Manipulation with Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18425)
*Jiaxi Huo,Petre Ricioppo,Fausto Francesco Lizzio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CALM是一个去中心化控制器，用于多无人机携带篮筐追踪移动地面机器人，结合MARL策略与多种估计、前馈模块，在风扰、载荷运动和噪声目标广播下表现优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同空中操作在风扰、载荷运动以及目标信息存在噪声的情况下十分困难，因为多架无人机通过共享载荷相互耦合，需要有效协调。

Method: CALM采用参数共享的多智能体强化学习（MARL）策略，使用MAPPO训练，并融合目标估计、每架无人机的扩展状态观测器（ESO）、篮筐质量估计以及前瞻性编队偏航前馈；前瞻前馈利用估计的目标运动提前旋转编队，减小篮筐偏航滞后且不增加净指令力；采用扰动条件化的critic支持集中训练、去中心化执行。

Result: 在留出轨迹上，CALM的平均位置和朝向误差低于所有评估基线，包括匹配机械臂基线（7.68 vs 10.95 cm，3.3 vs 4.7°）、缆索悬挂实现及两个基于模型的控制器；在未见过的全信息路径退化下，CALM与机械臂基线统计相当或更优。

Conclusion: CALM通过将MARL与估计和前馈机制结合，实现了鲁棒的去中心化协同空中操作，在追踪移动地面机器人任务中展现出优于基线的位置与朝向精度。

Abstract: Cooperative aerial manipulation is difficult under wind, payload motion, and noisy goal broadcasts because multiple Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are coupled through a shared payload. We present CALM (Cooperative Aerial Learned Manipulation), a decentralized controller for aerial manipulators carrying a basket while tracking a moving ground robot. CALM combines a parameter-shared Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) policy, trained using Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO), with goal estimation, per-UAV Extended State Observers (ESOs), basket-mass estimation, and anticipatory formation-yaw feedforward. The anticipatory formation-yaw feedforward uses estimated goal motion to rotate the formation before position error develops, reducing basket-yaw lag without adding net commanded force. A disturbance-conditioned critic supports centralized training while execution remains decentralized. Across held-out trajectories, CALM achieves lower mean position and orientation errors than all evaluated baselines, including the matched arm baseline (7.68 versus 10.95 cm and 3.3 versus 4.7$^\circ$), the cable-suspended realization, and two model-based controllers. Under unseen full-information-path degradation, CALM remains statistically comparable or superior to the arm baseline. Videos are available at https://petreric.github.io/CALM/.

</details>


### [226] [PACT: Posture-Aligned Co-Design Technique for Hand Configuration and Retargeting in Multi-Finger Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18443)
*Koyo Sasazaki,Satoshi Funabashi,Pranav Ponnivalavan,Alexander Schmitz,Tetsuya Ogata,Shigeki Sugano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACT 通过联合优化 17 自由度机器人手的机械参数与人手姿态重定向器，提升遥操作模仿学习的姿态保真度与抓取性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作示教中，机器人手能否忠实复现操作者姿态决定了模仿学习的数据质量。然而，姿态重定向规则通常在硬件定型后才确定，且传统手部设计往往忽视类人抓取姿态，导致姿态复现与抓取能力受限。

Method: 提出基于人手抓取姿态的优化方法 PACT，联合优化 17 舵机机器人手的机械参数（连杆长度、关节偏置、安装角、基座位置）与非线性姿态重定向器（线性人-机器人关节映射、逐关节姿态 IK 权重、阻尼）。优化以 DexYCB 数据集的人手姿态为目标，强制全手指对应（MCP、PIP、DIP 及指尖），并施加舵机干涉、装配间隙与平手突出约束，保证可实际装配。

Result: 制造 PACT Hand 并通过遥操作与模仿学习实验验证。全手关键点运动学 RMSE 为 3.9 mm，基线 LEAP Hand 为 12.8 mm；所有抓取姿态指尖误差均在 10 mm 内，基线仅 21.6% 姿态达标。相同逐关节力矩限制下，负载 2 kg（基线 1 kg），纸杯变形量仅为基线 1/7，接近人手水平；YCB 日常物体抓取成功率 86%（基线 64%）。基于 PACT Hand 示教训练的模仿学习策略在罐体取放任务中留出位置成功率达 75%。

Conclusion: 将机械设计与姿态重定向协同优化，可显著提升机器人手的类人姿态保真度、抓取鲁棒性与模仿学习效果，为遥操作数据采集与下游策略学习提供更可靠的硬件基础。

Abstract: The fidelity of teleoperated demonstrations used for imitation learning is determined by how faithfully a robot hand can replicate the operator's posture. The posture retargeting law that the robot will execute is typically selected only once the hardware has been finalized, and conventional hand designs often neglect human-like grasping postures. We introduce PACT, an optimization based on human grasping postures that jointly optimizes the mechanical parameters of a 17-servo robot hand (link lengths, joint offsets, mounting angles, and base placements) and its nonlinear posture retargeter (a linear human-to-robot joint map, per-joint posture-IK weights, and damping). The optimization enforces full-finger correspondence (MCP, PIP, DIP, and tip of each finger) to human poses from the DexYCB dataset, and is constrained by servo interference, assembly clearances, and flat-hand protrusion, so the resulting design can be physically assembled. We fabricate the PACT Hand and validate it through teleoperation experiments and an imitation-learning demonstration. The hand reproduces all human hand keypoints with a kinematic RMSE of 3.9 mm, compared to 12.8 mm for the LEAP Hand baseline, and keeps all fingertips within 10 mm on every grasping posture, while the baseline achieves this for only 21.6% of the poses. Under matched per-joint torque limits, it lifts twice the baseline payload, 2 kg against 1 kg, and deforms a held paper cup seven times less, reaching a deformation level comparable to a human hand. It also achieves an 86% grasping success rate on YCB daily objects, compared to 64% for the baseline. Finally, as a demonstration, an imitation-learning policy trained on demonstrations collected with the PACT Hand achieved a 75% success rate at held-out placements in a can pick-and-place task. Project page: https://pact-hand-2027.github.io

</details>


### [227] [MemoryVLN: From Ego-Aware Memory Representation to Cross-Context Memory Reuse in Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18478)
*Xueyao Wei,Yifei Wang,Delun Li,Shaoan Wang,Junzhi Yu,Wenjie Song,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MemoryVLN，一个以记忆构建与管理为核心的统一记忆驱动导航系统，用于提升长时程视觉语言导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在具身视觉语言导航中潜力巨大，但在长时程轨迹上因上下文长度限制、历史观测冗余、跨时间空间对齐不稳定而导致性能下降，难以压缩长导航历史并可靠复用过往空间经验。

Method: 在记忆构建阶段：长序列压缩采用受遗忘曲线启发的时间采样，在有限 token 预算下保留密集近期证据与稀疏但有代表性的远期观测；自我中心感知记忆表示融合 2D 语义 token、3D 几何 token 与 6D 智能体位姿，使记忆同时编码"看到了什么"与"在哪里看到的"。在记忆管理阶段：分层记忆库通过跨上下文热启动与置信度控制的写回机制维护回合内与上下文级记忆；记忆增强根据当前导航状态检索 Top-K 相关记忆，并与当前视觉 token 融合。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 与 RxR-CE 上的大量实验表明，相比当前最优基线取得一致且显著的提升，成功率分别提高 2% 和 6.5%。

Conclusion: 上述机制共同将原始导航历史转化为紧凑、空间有据、可选择性检索且可复用的导航经验，验证了记忆构建与管理在长时程 VLN 中的有效性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have shown strong potential for embodied vision-language navigation (VLN), yet their performance degrades on long-horizon trajectories due to context-length limits, redundant historical observations, and unstable spatial alignment across time. These issues make it difficult to both compress long navigation histories and reliably reuse past spatial experience. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{MemoryVLN}, a unified memory-driven navigation system built around memory construction and management. During memory construction, \emph{Long Sequence Compression} employs forgetting-curve-inspired temporal sampling to preserve dense recent evidence and sparse yet representative distant observations under a limited token budget. \emph{Ego-Aware Memory Representation} further integrates 2D semantic tokens, 3D geometric tokens, and 6D agent poses, allowing each memory to encode both what was observed and where it was observed. During memory management, \emph{Hierarchical Memory Bank} maintains in-episode and context-level memories through cross-context warm-start and confidence-controlled write-back. \emph{Memory Enhancement} then retrieves the Top-$K$ relevant memories according to the current navigation state and fuses them with the current visual tokens. Together, these mechanisms transform raw navigation history into compact, spatially grounded, selectively retrievable, and reusable navigation experience. Extensive experiments on R2R-CE and RxR-CE demonstrate consistent improvements over the state-of-the-art baselines by a substantial margin (i.e., 2\% and 6.5\% in successful rate).

</details>


### [228] [RTISSLAM: In-air Imaging Sonar SLAM using a Robust Pose Graph and Occupancy Grid Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18479)
*Jan Steckel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RTISSLAM，一种面向嵌入式3D成像声呐的纯声呐SLAM系统，结合BatSLAM 2.0位姿图后端与占据栅格地图，利用波束形成能量景观作为地点描述子实现回环检测，并在高镜面反射仿真环境中显著降低轨迹误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声呐是移动机器人早期建图的重要传感器，且在黑暗、烟雾、灰尘及玻璃附近等相机和激光雷达失效的场景中仍能工作。但传统声呐SLAM依赖几何特征或稀疏距离读数的占据栅格，难以应对室内声呐中普遍存在的镜面反射问题。

Method: RTISSLAM将BatSLAM 2.0的鲁棒位姿图作为后端，占据栅格作为地图；采用延迟乘加波束形成（DMAS）结合逐距离CFAR生成能量景观作为地点描述子，用于序列验证的回环检测；声学里程计在线校准轮式里程计；识别到的重访进行度量配准；栅格按关键帧存储并锚定到位姿图节点，随每次回环闭合更新。

Result: 在具有镜面墙壁的仿真世界中，RTISSLAM在办公室场景将轮式里程计的轨迹误差从8.2米降低到0.31米；而在相同声呐数据上，激光雷达风格的栅格SLAM失败。

Conclusion: RTISSLAM证明了纯声呐SLAM在镜面反射主导的室内环境中具有可行性，通过融合位姿图后端、能量景观地点描述子与在线声学里程计校准，能够实现高精度定位与建图，优于直接套用激光雷达式栅格SLAM的方法。

Abstract: Sonar was one of the first sensors with which mobile robots built maps, and in-air sonar keeps working where cameras and lidar fail, in darkness, smoke, dust and next to glass. Classical sonar SLAM, however, relied on geometric features or occupancy grids built from sparse range readings, and both struggle with the specular reflections that dominate indoor sonar. In this letter, we introduce RTISSLAM, a novel sonar-only SLAM system for an embedded 3D imaging sonar, which combines the robust pose graph of BatSLAM 2.0 as back-end with an occupancy grid as map. Energyscapes from delay-multiply-and-sum beamforming with a range-wise CFAR serve as place descriptors for sequence-verified loop closures, acoustic odometry calibrates the wheel odometry online, recognized revisits are registered metrically, and the grid is stored per keyframe and anchored to the pose-graph nodes, so that it follows every loop closure. In simulated worlds with specular walls, RTISSLAM reduces the trajectory error of the wheel odometry from \SI{8.2}{\meter} to \SI{0.31}{\meter} in an office, while lidar-style grid SLAM on the same sonar data fails.

</details>


### [229] [APTree: Extended Behavior Trees for Adaptive and Scalable Automated Robotic Task Planning in Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18523)
*Shermin Sherkat,Thomas Wortmann,Andreas Wortmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出APTree，一个模型驱动的框架，将自动化任务规划与基于行为树的模块化任务执行相结合，通过协调异构符号规划器在运行时生成和组合模块化任务计划，并在真实建筑预制场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人异地施工预制的任务规划主要依赖人工，导致规划效率低下，且业界对采用机器人解决方案持犹豫态度。现有方法对可扩展、模块化的自动化任务规划以及本地化运行时重规划的支持有限。

Method: APTree以行为树作为执行模型，反复应用并协调异构符号规划器，在运行时自动生成和组合模块化任务计划。为支撑该方式，作者开发了一种领域特定语言用于任务计划的形式化规范与验证，并配套开发了兼容的执行引擎。

Result: 在真实的livMatS仿生贝壳展馆上进行评估：APTree对建筑机器人制造领域建模，在设计时和运行时检测注入的规范与规划错误，并通过协调三个符号规划器、跨多个规划模块自动生成大规模任务计划。机器校验的形式化分析进一步表明，在明确假设（规划器可靠性与生成计划无干扰）下，组合执行与本地重规划是安全的。

Conclusion: APTree使建筑从业者无需深入了解符号规划技术或APTree内部实现，即可利用自动化任务规划，为可靠的计划组合提供了明确条件。

Abstract: Task planning for robots in off-site construction prefabrication is primarily manual, leading to planning inefficiencies and reluctance to adopt robotic solutions. Existing approaches provide limited support for scalable, modular automated task planning and localized runtime replanning. This paper proposes APTree, a model-driven framework that integrates automated task planning with modular task execution using behavior trees as the execution model. APTree repeatedly applies and coordinates heterogeneous symbolic planners to automatically generate and compose modular task plans at runtime. To support this approach, we develop a domain-specific language for the formal specification and validation of task plans, together with a compatible execution engine. We evaluate APTree on the real-world livMatS Biomimetic Shell pavilion, where it models the construction robotic fabrication domain, detects injected specification and planning errors at design time and runtime, and automatically generates large-scale task plans by coordinating three symbolic planners across numerous planning modules. A machine-checked formal analysis further shows that composed execution and local replanning are safe under explicitly stated assumptions, including planner soundness and non-interference of generated plans, providing practitioners with explicit conditions for dependable plan composition. APTree thus enables construction practitioners to leverage automated task planning without requiring detailed knowledge of symbolic planning techniques or APTree's internal implementation.

</details>


### [230] [Behavioural self-monitoring for autonomous earth observation agents: The attribute reliability monitor (ARM)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18548)
*Alejandro D. Mousist*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"属性可靠性监控器"（ARM），通过随时间累积怀疑证据来评估模型的单个语义属性可靠性，并在卫星热红外影像的视觉语言模型上进行了初步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言与视觉语言模型可支撑自主系统，但运行条件变化可能削弱其语义输出的可靠性，因此需要一种机制来实时评估输出可信度。

Method: ARM 采用黑盒监控方式，仅依赖可观测的模型响应，不访问内部状态、置信度分数或外部参考答案；它可兼容不同检测器与时间监控方法，并将干预策略留给使用系统自行决定。

Result: 在可控输入退化条件下，模型响应之间的不一致为选择性暂停某些属性提供了证据，实现了对属性可靠性的动态评估。

Conclusion: 外部行为监控可支持模型输出的持续使用，并能区分"运行层面的怀疑"与"语义正确性"，为自主系统在高风险场景中的安全应用提供了参考。

Abstract: Language and vision language models can support autonomous systems, but changing operating conditions may compromise the reliability of their semantic outputs. This work introduces the Attribute Reliability Monitor (ARM), a framework that accumulates evidence of suspicion over time to assess individual semantic attributes. ARM accommodates different detectors and temporal monitoring methods, while leaving intervention policies to the consuming system. An initial implementation was evaluated using a specialized vision language model developed for satellite thermal infrared imagery. Monitoring followed a black box approach, using only observable responses without access to internal model states, confidence scores, or external reference answers. Under controlled input degradation, disagreement between responses provided evidence for selective suspension of attributes. The results illustrate how external behavioral monitoring can inform continued use of model outputs, while distinguishing operational suspicion from semantic correctness.

</details>


### [231] [WAVE-0: Bridging VLA Policies and WAMs with Lightweight Latent Future Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18568)
*Haomin Wang,Anqiong Ji,Ke Wang,Zhongshan Liu,Ru Ying,Ran Zheng,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WAVE-0 通过在预训练 VLA 上接入轻量潜在世界模型，让预测的动作后果直接引导动作生成，无需像素重建或大规模视频预训练，在仿真与真实任务上均取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略虽具备通用操作能力，但生成动作时未显式建模其对环境的后果；而世界动作模型（WAM）虽引入未来预测，却多依赖昂贵的视频生成，或仅把预测未来当作辅助目标，抑或需通过大规模视频预训练从次优视觉过渡中推断潜在动作，代价高且效果受限。

Method: 提出 WAVE-0 框架，用轻量潜在世界模型连接 VLA 与 WAM。基于预训练 VLA，其 257M 世界模型将 VLM 生成的潜在动作计划转化为预测的未来表示，用以条件化动作专家，统一潜在规划、未来预测与动作生成；世界模型直接以真值机器人动作训练，采用反事实排序目标，无需像素重建和大规模视频预训练。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 上成功率 91.47%，LIBERO 上 98.7%；LIBERO-Plus、LIBERO-Pro、LIBERO-Para 分别达 88.0%、61.4%、75.0%。真实任务中把 π_{0.5} 的成功率从 38.3% 提升至 73.3%。

Conclusion: WAVE-0 通过轻量潜在世界模型让预测后果直接指导动作生成，在多种分布偏移下展现出强操作性能与泛化能力，且训练成本较低。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies excel at generalist robotic manipulation but generate actions without explicitly modeling their environmental consequences. World action models (WAMs) incorporate future prediction, yet most rely on costly video generation, while latent alternatives either use predicted futures only as auxiliary targets or infer latent actions from suboptimal visual transitions through costly large-scale video pretraining. We introduce WAVE-0 (World-model-Augmented VLA Execution), a framework that bridges VLAs and WAMs through a lightweight latent world model. By connecting the semantic understanding of pretrained VLAs with explicit future prediction, WAVE-0 allows anticipated action consequences to directly guide action generation. Built on a pretrained VLA, its 257M world model translates VLM-generated latent action plans into predicted future representations that condition the action expert, unifying latent planning, future prediction, and action generation. The world model is trained directly on ground-truth robot actions with a counterfactual ranking objective, requiring neither pixel reconstruction nor large-scale video pretraining. WAVE-0 achieves success rates of 91.47% on RoboTwin 2.0 and 98.7% on LIBERO, along with 88.0%, 61.4%, and 75.0% on LIBERO-Plus, LIBERO-Pro, and LIBERO-Para, respectively. On real-world tasks, it improves the success rate of $π_{0.5}$ from 38.3% to 73.3%. These results demonstrate strong manipulation performance and generalization across diverse distribution shifts.

</details>


### [232] [ACCRUE: Continual Off-Policy Reinforcement Learning for Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18596)
*Changyeon Kim,Kimin Lee,Yashraj Narang,Yijie Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ACCRUE，一种连续离策略强化学习框架，通过跨任务继承轻量actor和critic网络实现高效新任务学习与近零遗忘，在实际机器人上减少31.0%~68.5%训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型部署需要既能高效学习新任务又能保留已获得行为。现有连续RL方法（基于VLA）虽能缓解遗忘，但依赖在策略更新且前向迁移有限；离策略方法虽提升单任务样本效率，却未解决跨任务的学习者持续性问题。

Method: ACCRUE在任务间传递轻量actor和critic网络。继承的critic仅在当前任务转移上适应并指导当前任务actor学习；通过在小量保留回合上将actor蒸馏至缓存目标来保留先前行为。固定视觉特征使继承的学习器及其回放与基础策略更新保持兼容。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人硬件的接触密集型装配序列上，ACCRUE加速后续任务学习，遗忘接近零，并将实际机器人训练时间减少31.0%至68.5%。

Conclusion: ACCRUE通过连续离策略RL跨任务继承actor与critic，兼顾新任务高效学习与旧行为保持，验证了在真实机器人装配任务中的显著效率提升与近乎无遗忘表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves robot foundation models (RFMs) beyond their demonstrations, but real-world deployment demands more: learning new tasks efficiently while retaining previously acquired behaviors. Existing continual RL methods on top of VLAs mitigate forgetting but rely on on-policy updates with limited forward transfer, whereas off-policy methods improve single-task sample efficiency without addressing learner persistence across tasks. We propose ACCRUE, a continual off-policy RL framework that carries lightweight actor and critic networks across tasks, allowing each stage to reuse behavioral and value knowledge acquired through prior RL. The inherited critic adapts only on current-task transitions and guides current-task actor learning, while prior behavior is preserved by distilling the actor toward cached targets on a small number of retained episodes. Fixed visual features keep the inherited learner and its replay compatible with base-policy updates. Across contact-rich assembly sequences in simulation and on real-robot hardware, ACCRUE accelerates subsequent-task learning with near-zero forgetting and significantly reduces real-robot training time by 31.0\% to 68.5\%.

</details>


### [233] [Grid or Nodes? Probing Object Memory in World Models Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18637)
*Justin McMillen,Yasin Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文在FloWM自身的积木世界中对比了基于特征空间网格的学习式世界模型(FloWM)与基于显式位置速度数值的节点记忆(以Kalman跟踪器为oracle)，发现后者在视野外物体追踪上以极小内存(14KB vs 5.6MB)达到相当性能，并在对角运动等超出FloWM内置速度的情形下显著更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人世界模型必须记住离开相机视野的物体。FloWM将物体保存在随五个内置速度移动的特征空间网格中，而节点记忆则用数值保存每个物体的位置与速度。作者希望在FloWM自己的积木世界设定下，直接比较这两种记忆范式的优劣。

Method: 在FloWM的积木世界中进行对比实验，采用oracle-fed Kalman跟踪器作为节点记忆。测试不同网格读取方式（单格探针 vs FloWM自带探针）对得分的影响，比较视野内与视野外表现、内存占用，以及对角运动与更高速运动下的鲁棒性，并考察FloWM微调后的效果。

Result: 网格读取方式影响得分：单格探针在视野外找到66%的积木，FloWM自带探针为89%。FloWM在未展示任何帧的视野内情形仍高出8分，归因于其自身预测的回写。视野外，跟踪器以14KB（对5.6MB）达到FloWM的量级且无需探针。对角运动下，FloWM即使微调后也只找到11%的积木，而未改动的跟踪器保持93%。更快的积木使两者都失效，直到允许一个额外速度后跟踪器达到94%，而此时FloWM的内置位移需要25份网格拷贝才能实现。

Conclusion: 节点记忆在显式建模位置与速度方面优于基于网格特征的学习式世界模型，尤其体现在内存效率、视野外追踪、超出内置速度的运动以及高速运动场景。未来需要一个学习式节点记忆来替代oracle，并处理噪声、长间隙与弹跳下的速度估计问题。

Abstract: A robot's world model must remember objects that leave the camera's view. FloWM, a learned world model, keeps them in a spatial grid of features that it moves with five built-in velocities. A node memory instead keeps each object's position and velocity as numbers. We compare them in FloWM's own block world, with an oracle-fed Kalman tracker as the node memory. How the grid is read changes its score: a single-cell probe finds 66% of out-of-view blocks, while FloWM's own probe 89%. FloWM also scores 8 points higher in view although no frame is shown, which we attribute to its write-back of its own predictions. Out of view, the tracker reaches FloWM's range in 14KB against 5.6MB, with no probe. On diagonal motion, outside its five velocities, FloWM finds 11% of blocks even after fine-tuning, while the unchanged tracker keeps 93%. Faster blocks defeat both until one more allowed speed takes the tracker to 94%, where FloWM's built-in shifts would need 25 copies of its grid. A learned node memory must still replace the oracles and handle velocity under noise, long gaps, and the bounce.

</details>


### [234] [ForkCast: Predictive Recovery for Vision-Language-Action Policies via Counterfactual Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18650)
*Chenru Wen,Huajian Zeng,Haokai Ding,Jian Kang,Anqing Duan,Dawei Zhou,Dezhen Song,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForkCast 是一种预测性恢复框架，通过对比反事实未来来决定是否以及如何干预冻结的 VLA 策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略在执行操作任务时，物体滑落或视觉遮挡会打断执行。传统恢复方法要么依赖策略训练时学习，要么依赖监控器检测失败后触发脚本化修复。作者希望无需失败检测器和干预阈值，也能对冻结的 VLA 策略进行有效干预。

Method: ForkCast 在每个决策点评估三个候选动作：执行策略的名义动作块（Commit）、调整相机视角（Reobserve）、重规划机械臂轨迹（Replan）。使用候选条件世界模型在统一未来时域上预测各候选的潜在结果，再用共享任务进度头对预测结果打分。通过将策略自身延续与修复候选一起比较，基于任务进度的单次比较同时决定是否干预和如何干预，无需更新 VLA 主干。

Result: 在五个注入遮挡和物体滑落的 RoboTwin 2.0 仿真任务上，ForkCast 将冻结 π_{0.5} 策略的汇总成功率从 13.3% 提升至 30.0%，并能迁移到另外两个 VLA 主干而无需更新 ForkCast。在真实机器人候选轨迹上适应后，ForkCast 在干净和受限可见性条件下均优于基础策略。

Conclusion: ForkCast 通过反事实未来比较实现了无需失败检测器和阈值更新的预测性恢复，在仿真和真实机器人上均提升了 VLA 策略的成功率，并具备跨主干迁移能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies perform manipulation tasks using camera images and language instructions, but object slip or visual occlusion can disrupt execution. Recovery is typically learned during policy training or delegated to a monitor that triggers a scripted repair after detecting a failure. We introduce ForkCast, a predictive recovery framework that compares counterfactual futures to determine whether and how to intervene in a frozen VLA policy. At each decision point, ForkCast evaluates three candidates: executing the policy's nominal action chunk (Commit), adjusting the camera viewpoint (Reobserve), and replanning the arm trajectory (Replan). A candidate-conditioned world model forecasts the latent outcome of each candidate at a common future horizon, and a shared task-progress head scores the predicted outcomes. By evaluating the policy's own continuation alongside the repair candidates, a single comparison based on task progress determines both whether and how to intervene, without a failure detector, an intervention threshold, or updates to the VLA backbone. On five simulated RoboTwin 2.0 tasks with injected occlusion and object slip, ForkCast increases pooled success from 13.3% for the frozen $π_{0.5}$ policy to 30.0% and transfers to two additional VLA backbones without updating ForkCast. After adaptation on real-robot candidate rollouts, ForkCast also improves success over the base policy under both clean and visibility-limited conditions. Code will be released to benefit the research community.

</details>


### [235] [Perception Beyond the Visible: Incompleteness-Aware Scene Understanding for Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18670)
*Rohit Mohan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"不完整性感知的场景理解"研究愿景，围绕不完整证据、不完整知识、不完整传感器可靠性三大挑战展开，并展望以信念为中心的4D场景表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的机器人自主性需在部分且含噪的观测下推理世界，但现有感知系统仅建模可见区域、使用固定语义类别、假设传感器测量可靠，这些假设在遮挡、新物体和传感器退化时失效。

Method: 采用研究愿景/立场论述方式，组织为三大挑战（不完整证据、不完整知识、不完整传感器可靠性），并通过非模态感知、语义占用预测、开放世界场景理解、标签高效学习和不确定性引导的多模态融合等方向加以应对。

Result: 本文为愿景性工作，未给出具体实验结果，主要梳理作者在相关方向的研究贡献并据此提出未来方向。

Conclusion: 未来应构建统一的以信念为中心的4D场景表示，联合建模几何、语义、运动与不确定性，并结合主动感知与安全自适应策略，形成可校准、可行动的表示以支撑动态开放世界中的可靠自主性。

Abstract: Reliable robot autonomy requires reasoning about the world from partial and noisy observations. However, many perception systems model only visible scene regions, rely on fixed semantic categories, and assume reliable sensor measurements. These assumptions break under occlusion, novel object encounters, and sensor degradation. In this paper, I present a research vision for incompleteness-aware scene understanding. I organize this vision around three fundamental challenges: incomplete evidence, incomplete knowledge, and incomplete sensor reliability. I discuss my research contributions toward addressing these challenges through amodal perception, semantic occupancy forecasting, open-world scene understanding, label-efficient learning, and uncertainty-guided multi-modal fusion. Building on these contributions, I outline future research directions toward belief-centric 4D scene representations. These representations aim to jointly model geometry, semantics, motion, and uncertainty. I also discuss active sensing and safe adaptation strategies that allow robots to reduce uncertainty through interaction. The goal is to develop calibrated and actionable scene representations that support reliable autonomy in dynamic, open-world environments.

</details>


### [236] [UniW(A)M: A Unified Mixture-of-Experts Framework for World and World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18697)
*Jingbo Xu,Jianjie Fang,Zhuohang Li,Zihao Song,Zhaolu Wang,Xvyuan Liu,Xin Zhang,Haisheng Su,Yinzhou Tang,Chen Gao,Wei Wu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 UniW(A)M，一种基于扩散 Transformer 的专家混合世界（动作）模型，统一动作条件世界建模与多种世界-动作模型变体，并通过即插即用自适应接口路由器实现高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型（WM）侧重未来世界预测，世界-动作模型（WAM）侧重可执行动作生成，二者虽共享具身轨迹中的物理动力学与交互语义，却通常被孤立训练，限制了预测与控制之间的知识复用。核心挑战在于构建统一的学范框架，在共享动力学的同时保留各范式所需的差异化建模能力。

Method: UniW(A)M 基于扩散 Transformer 构建专家混合架构：共享专家捕捉通用动力学，原子能力专家在各接口间复用，同时每个接口保留自身的条件通路；部署阶段引入即插即用自适应接口路由器（PAIR），从少量目标任务 chunk 场景中学习，为每个执行 chunk 选择合适的 WAM 接口，在冻结主干的同时平衡任务成功率与执行效率。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0 及三个真实世界操作任务上评估，真实世界平均成功率达 88.3%，最强基线为 76.7%；在 WorldArena 上三项任务的动作条件世界建模均领先。AC-WM 协同训练对每个动作接口的动作生成均有增益，PAIR 将 OOD 成功率从 63.3% 提升至 76.7%。

Conclusion: UniW(A)M 验证了统一世界建模与动作生成、共享动力学与保留接口特异能力的可行性，协同训练与自适应路由分别提升了动作生成与分布外泛化性能。

Abstract: World models (WMs) and world-action models (WAMs) focus on future world prediction and executable action generation, respectively. Although these models learn shared physical dynamics and interaction semantics from embodied trajectories, they are typically trained in isolation, which restricts knowledge reuse between prediction and control. The central challenge is therefore to develop a unified learning framework that shares these dynamics while preserving the distinct modeling capabilities required by each paradigm.
  We propose UniW(A)M, a mixture-of-experts world-(action) model built upon diffusion Transformers that unifies action-conditioned world modeling and diverse WAM variants. Shared experts capture common dynamics, while atomic capability experts are reused across interfaces so that each interface keeps its own conditional pathway. At deployment, a plug-in adaptive interface router (PAIR) learns from a small subset of target-task chunk scenarios to select a suitable WAM interface for each execution chunk, balancing task success and execution efficiency while keeping the backbone frozen.
  We evaluate UniW(A)M on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and three real-world manipulation tasks, where it achieves 88.3% average real-world success versus 76.7% for the strongest baseline. On WorldArena, it leads action-conditioned world modeling on all three tasks. AC-WM co-training contributes to action generation on every action interface and PAIR raises OOD success from 63.3% to 76.7%.

</details>


### [237] [RoboFQE: Rollout-Free Evaluation of Visual Robot Policies from Offline Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18699)
*Yunfu Deng,Sangwoo Shin,Brahma S. Pavse,Josiah P. Hanna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboFQE，一种离策略评估方法，无需真实执行、仿真器或世界模型，即可从离线轨迹估计目标策略的折扣期望回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人策略评估依赖真实执行、仿真器或学习到的世界模型生成新轨迹，基础设施要求高，且后两者存在预测误差随评估时域累积的问题。作者希望研究离策略评估作为替代方案。

Method: 将 fitted Q-evaluation 与预训练视觉基础模型及学习到的终端成功标注相结合，无需估计行为策略密度，从而弥合标准 OPE 基准与连续控制视觉操作任务之间的差距。

Result: 在仿真与真实操作任务的对照实验中，RoboFQE 能产生准确的折扣回报估计并保持策略排序，尤其在长时域任务中优于受自回归预测误差影响的视频世界模型评估器。消融实验显示预训练视觉编码器显著优于从零训练方法，学习到的终端成功标注可在无真实奖励标签下评估，仅轻微降低精度。

Conclusion: 离策略评估可作为基于 rollout 的机器人策略评估的有效替代，RoboFQE 在长时域视觉操作任务中展现出准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Evaluating robot policies before deployment typically requires producing new trajectories through real-world execution, simulators, or learned world models. These rollout-based approaches impose infrastructure requirements; simulator and world-model evaluators additionally face prediction errors that can compound over the evaluation horizon. In this paper, we study off-policy evaluation as an alternative to rollout-based robot-policy evaluation methods. Specifically, we introduce RoboFQE, an evaluator that estimates the discounted expected return of a target policy from offline robot trajectories without estimating a behavior-policy density, combining fitted Q-evaluation with a pretrained vision foundation model and learned terminal success annotation to bridge the gap between standard OPE benchmarks and continuous-control visual manipulation. Controlled experiments across simulated and real-world manipulation tasks show that RoboFQE produces accurate discounted-return estimates and preserves policy rankings even on long-horizon manipulation tasks where video world-model evaluators are degraded by autoregressive prediction errors. Ablation experiments show that pretrained vision encoders substantially outperform from-scratch alternatives, particularly on long-horizon tasks, and that learned terminal success annotation enables evaluation without ground-truth reward labels with only slight reduction of accuracy.

</details>


### [238] [Steertuning Policies from a Few Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18711)
*Billy Yan,Isaac Tu,Kelly Lee,Blake Chang,Kenny Ma,Kanad Patel,Dhawal Kabra,Neer Patel,Kevin Wu,Lerrel Pinto,Irmak Guzey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Steertuning使机器人仅用5个人类演示、5分钟内即可在新环境学习新任务，并在12个新环境的12个任务中取得71%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习方法需要大量机器人演示才能获得稳健且自适应的策略，数据成本高、部署周期长。本文旨在用极少量人类演示实现快速、泛化的新任务与新环境学习。

Method: 方法分两部分：(1) 在100小时密集标注的自我中心人类随机交互数据集上预训练4亿参数扩散策略，使其对背景差异稳健并具备空间与物体泛化能力；(2) 给定新任务的5个演示，仅微调2分钟（2000次更新），并在反向采样过程中用观测最接近部署环境的演示来引导策略动作；同时设计面向具身迁移的数据处理流程。

Result: 在12个新环境（从实验室到新家厨房）的12个任务中达到71%成功率；从新任务数据采集到机器人策略部署全程不到5分钟。

Conclusion: Steertuning通过大规模人类交互预训练结合少量演示微调与采样引导，实现了高效、可泛化的机器人学习，大幅降低演示与部署成本；数据集、模型权重、代码和视频将公开。

Abstract: Current recipes for robot learning require massive amounts of robot demonstrations to obtain robust and adaptive policies. In this work, we introduce Steertuning, a system that enables robots to learn new tasks in new environments from 5 human demonstrations in under 5
  Steertuning consists of two parts. (1) We pretrain a 400M-parameter diffusion policy on our egocentric human dataset, which consists of 100 hours of densely annotated random interactions. This pretraining makes Steertuning robust to background differences and enables spatial and object generalization when deployed on new tasks. (2) Given 5 demonstrations of a new task, we finetune our model for only 2 minutes (2000 training updates) and steer the policy's actions during reverse sampling using the demonstration whose observation is closest to that of the deployment environment. Together with a carefully designed data processing pipeline for embodiment transfer, this enables Steertuning to achieve a 71% success rate across 12 tasks in 12 new environments, ranging from lab settings to kitchens in new homes. The entire process, from data collection for a new task to robot policy deployment, takes less than 5 minutes. Our full dataset, model weights, codebase, and robot videos will be made available at our website: https://steertuning.github.io.

</details>


### [239] [GreenWAM: A World Foundation Model for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18774)
*Gennady Sidorov,Valerii Kornilov,Denis Grankin,Polina Fedotova,Aleksey Postnikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GreenWAM 是一个机器人世界基础模型，同时充当世界模型与策略，通过共享解剖动作空间、多模式联合训练和动作专家分离，在仿真与真实人形机器人上均取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人既需要预测动作如何改变场景的世界模型，也需要选择动作的策略，但现有模型通常只针对其中一种角色构建和评估，且各机器人记录动作的格式不统一。论文因此追问：一个世界模型能否跨不同本体同时服务这两种角色，以及其中的关键因素是什么。

Method: 方法包含三个部分：(1) 共享解剖动作空间，用统一关节坐标描述 20 个机器人家族，覆盖双臂机械臂到灵巧人形；(2) 在这些家族上以视觉、动作条件和世界-动作三种模式联合训练；(3) 从生成器中分离出动作专家以构成策略。作者还将其适配到真实人形机器人，并蒸馏为单步策略用于实时控制。

Result: 作为世界模型，GreenWAM 在 WorldArena 1.0 上取得顶尖的轨迹、深度和 3D 精度，超过所有对比的世界-动作模型与视频生成器。作为策略，它进入 RoboTwin 2.0 排行榜前列，在 LIBERO-plus 上取得对比方法中最高的零样本成功率，并在 CALVIN 和 LIBERO 上与专用 VLA 和世界-动作策略相当。在真实人形机器人上成功率高于从 π0.5 和 Cosmos 3 Edge 适配的策略。受控实验得出三点结论：机器人专门化同时改善世界预测与控制；动作专家在适配开始时分离更有效；策略适配期间保留世界模型模式可保持对场景变化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 一个机器人世界模型可以跨不同本体同时充当世界模型与策略，其关键在于共享动作空间、多模式联合训练与动作专家分离，且专门化和保留世界模型模式对预测、控制与鲁棒性均有帮助。

Abstract: A robot needs a world model that predicts how its actions will change the scene and a policy that chooses those actions. Models are usually built and evaluated for only one of these roles, and every robot records its actions in its own format. We ask whether one robot world model can serve both roles across different bodies, and what makes this work. We present GreenWAM, a world foundation model for robotics, together with the recipe that produces it: (1) a shared anatomical action space that describes 20 robot families, from bimanual arms to dexterous humanoids, in common joint coordinates; (2) joint training on these families in visual, action-conditioned and world-action modes; and (3) policies that split an action expert off the generator. As a world model, GreenWAM attains top-tier trajectory, depth and 3D accuracy on WorldArena 1.0, ahead of every world-action model and video generator in our comparison. As a policy, it scores among the top entries of the RoboTwin 2.0 leaderboard, reaches the highest zero-shot LIBERO-plus success among the compared methods and is competitive with dedicated vision-language-action and world-action policies on CALVIN and LIBERO. We further adapt GreenWAM to a real humanoid, where it succeeds more often than policies adapted from $π_{0.5}$ and from Cosmos 3 Edge, and distill it into a single-step policy for real-time control. Controlled experiments yield three findings: robot specialization improves world prediction and control together; an action expert helps when split off at the start of adaptation; and keeping world-model modes during policy adaptation preserves robustness to scene changes.

</details>


### [240] [EqC: Equilibrium Energy Control for Introspective Lifelong Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18790)
*Haodong Yang,Runhan Huang,Bowen Zhang,Iofai Lei,Zunzhe Zhang,Xiangcheng Zhang,Shaoting Zhu,Linzhan Mou,Yicheng Liu,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Equilibrium Energy Control (EqC) 框架，通过显式条件能量景观实现内省式终身机器人学习，并借助 Energy-Gated DAgger 在能力边界处触发专家干预，从而在仿真与真实双臂操作任务中提升性能与自主性范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期机器人自主性不仅依赖有效的动作生成，还需要策略能够识别自身行为何时不再获得充分支持，并借助外部协助来改进。因此需要一种能够"内省"的终身学习机制，在策略接近能力边界时及时发现并触发干预。

Method: 提出 EqC 框架，构建显式的条件能量景观，将生成式动作精炼视为平衡态求解优化，并通过精炼过程的几何性质判断生成行为是否与学习到的能量景观保持一致。该一致性信号用于 Energy-Gated DAgger：当持续出现能量一致性违规时触发专家接管，并将恢复轨迹聚合用于后续策略更新。EqC 可自然集成到 Diffusion Transformer 策略和基于流匹配的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界双臂操作任务中，EqC 达到或优于现有方法的控制性能与鲁棒性，同时能够在策略能力边界附近及时触发干预。真实世界研究进一步表明，这些干预逐步扩展了系统可自主处理的情境范围。

Conclusion: EqC 提供了一条从静态生成式控制器走向自我改进机器人系统的实用路径，验证了内省式能力评估与专家干预相结合对于终身机器人学习的有效性。

Abstract: Long-term robot autonomy depends on more than effective action generation: a policy must recognize when its behavior is no longer sufficiently supported and use assistance to improve. We introduce Equilibrium Energy Control (EqC), a framework for introspective lifelong robot learning built around an explicit conditional energy landscape. EqC casts generative action refinement as equilibrium-seeking optimization, while the geometry of the refinement process reveals whether the generated behavior remains consistent with the learned landscape. This competence signal enables Energy-Gated DAgger, where persistent energy-consistency violations trigger expert takeover and the resulting recovery trajectories are aggregated for subsequent policy updates. EqC integrates naturally into both Diffusion Transformer policies and flow-matching-based vision-language-action (VLA) policies. Across simulation and real-world bimanual manipulation, EqC matches or improves control performance and robustness while enabling timely intervention near the policy's competence boundary. A real-world study further shows how these interventions progressively expand the range of situations handled autonomously, providing a practical path from static generative controllers toward self-improving robot systems.

</details>


### [241] [SEAR: Self-Evolving Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18792)
*Xinyi Wang,Bangzheng Li,Hanyu Wang,Lin Zhang,Andrea Yaoyun Cui,Zhenggang Tang,Shyamal Buch,Dejia Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SEAR框架与基准，用于评估语言模型机器人智能体是否真正具备自我进化能力，并在三种运行模式、七个模型、330个仿真任务和5个真实任务上进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型智能体已能自主改进机器人（编写控制代码、测试、保留有效技能与记忆），但现有研究难以区分智能体是真正在学习，还是仅仅更频繁地尝试；同时也不清楚所学内容能否迁移到新任务、新模拟器和真实机器人上。因此需要一个能分离这些因素的框架与基准。

Method: 提出SEAR（Self-Evolving Agentic Robotics）框架与基准。框架支持三种运行模式：1）code-as-policy，编写代码成为策略；2）agent-as-policy，实时参与控制循环；3）auto-research，作为研究者微调视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型。基准设计了对照条件，以区分保留经验与重复尝试、熟悉任务、特权模拟器状态、更大预算和运气等混淆因素。

Result: 在七个前沿语言模型、330个仿真任务和5个物理任务、超过四年的智能体运行时长上得到三点发现：1）学习曲线取决于总时间预算和任务相对模型能力的难度，困难任务值得更长会话，简单任务重新短尝试效果相近；2）控制代码和共享例程可跨任务和模拟器迁移，任务特定方案则不能，真实机器人上智能体设计的练习与新人类设计场景间存在性能差距；3）不同模型进化方式不同，有的擅长实时执行，有的擅长先写代码，且纯文本模型可与多模态模型媲美——感知有助于错误评估，但智能体推理与工具使用可能更重要。

Conclusion: SEAR框架与基准揭示了机器人智能体自我进化的条件性与差异性，强调推理与工具使用的重要性，并开源基准与代码库以支持未来研究。

Abstract: Frontier language-model agents can now improve robots on their own: they write control code, test it, and keep what works as skills and memories for the next task. We introduce SEAR (Self-Evolving Agentic Robotics), a framework and benchmark built around two questions. Is the agent really learning, or just trying more often? And does what it learns still help on new tasks, in a new simulator, and on a real robot? The framework runs an agent in three modes: it writes code that becomes the policy (code-as-policy), acts live in the control loop (agent-as-policy), or works as a researcher that fine-tunes a vision-language-action model (auto-research). The benchmark adds controls that separate retained experience from repeated attempts, familiar tasks, privileged simulator state, larger budgets, and luck. Across seven frontier language models, 330 simulated and five physical tasks, and over four years of agent runtime, we highlight three findings. First, the learning curve of a robotic agent depends both on the overall time budget and the task difficulty relative to model capability. Harder tasks make longer sessions worthwhile; on easier tasks, re-rolling short attempts does just as well. Second, control code and shared routines transfer across tasks and simulators, while task-specific solutions do not. On a real robot, we observe a performance gap between agent-designed practice and novel human-designed scenes. Third, different models evolve more effectively in different ways. Some do best acting live, others writing code first. And a text-only model keeps pace with a comparable multimodal one: perception helps with error assessment, but agentic reasoning and tool use can matter more. We release the benchmark and codebase to support future research.

</details>


### [242] [EgoMatrix: Physically Faithful Dexterous Robot Demonstrations from Egocentric Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18822)
*Ziheng Wang,Yanming Shao,Ting Mao,Kaihan Chen,Yiqun Wang,Xuanye Wu,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoMatrix：将单目第一人称人类视频端到端转换为双臂灵巧操作轨迹及配对机器人观测视频，可在真机回放，仿真端到端成功率54.84%（基线14.52%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称人类视频是机器人遥操作之外可扩展的灵巧操作数据来源，但存在两个瓶颈：单目RGB下精确的手-物交互（HOI）重建困难，以及难以获得高质量机器人演示（尤其视觉观测）。

Method: 端到端数据引擎：重建HOI时侧重相对几何与接触而非精确度量场景恢复；再用重定向与强化学习（RL）在仿真中复现任务；联合渲染机器人手与物体，生成与状态和控制记录同步的物理可信观测；并将同一轨迹在多样且任务兼容的背景中渲染以扩充配对数据，同时保持机-物运动与接触。

Result: 在TACO、OakInk2及自采数据上，仿真端到端成功率54.84%，最强基线为14.52%；重建在两个公开基准的多数指标上达到SOTA；定性结果显示手-物交互比已有方法更真实；特征空间分析显示其与真实机器人观测的对齐优于GPT-6 Astra。

Conclusion: EgoMatrix通过弱化度量精度、强调几何与接触一致性，并借助重定向、RL与联合渲染，把人类视频转化为可回放、可扩充的机器人灵巧操作训练数据，提升了下游任务成功率与数据真实性。

Abstract: Egocentric human videos offer a scalable alternative to robot teleoperation for collecting dexterous manipulation data. However, two challenges limit their use as reliable training data: accurate hand-object interaction (HOI) reconstruction from monocular RGB and high-quality robot demonstrations, especially visual observations. We present EgoMatrix, an end-to-end data engine that converts monocular egocentric human videos into bimanual dexterous trajectories and paired robot observation videos, with the trajectories directly replayable on real robots. It reconstructs HOI with emphasis on relative geometry and contact rather than exact metric scene recovery, then uses retargeting and reinforcement learning (RL) to reproduce the demonstrated tasks in simulation. We jointly render robot hands and objects from these simulated trajectories to produce physically faithful observations synchronized with state and control records. Rendering the same trajectories in diverse, task-compatible backgrounds expands paired training data while preserving robot-object motion and contact. Across TACO, OakInk2, and self-collected data, EgoMatrix achieves 54.84% end-to-end success in simulation versus 14.52% for the strongest baseline. Reconstruction reaches state-of-the-art performance on most metrics of the two public benchmarks. Qualitative results show more faithful hand-object interactions than prior methods, while feature-space analysis shows closer alignment with real-robot observations than GPT-6 Astra.Our project page is available at https://spikew726.github.io/EgoMatrix/.

</details>


### [243] [WorldOracle: Benchmarking Action-Conditioned World Models against Real-World Executions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18826)
*Haoyi Duan,Liliang Chen,Nan Wang,Yang Zhang,Yuhang Lu,Xiaotong Chen,Wei-Cheng Tseng,Shenyuan Gao,Zhaoshuo Li,Jingyi Jin,Pooya Jannaty,Dhruv Shah,Yuke Zhu,Ming-Yu Liu,Maoqing Yao,Guanghui Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldOracle基准，用于以配对真实世界执行为参照，评估动作条件世界模型（ACWMs）的预测展开是否与真实世界一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型缺乏与真实世界执行直接配对的系统性评估手段，难以衡量其预测展开在物理后果和任务状态转换上的真实性。

Method: 构建WorldOracle基准：给定相同初始观测与低层动作序列，从四个维度评估预测展开，包括物理状态正确性、交互空间对齐、关键交互区域保真度和多视角动作一致性；并整理覆盖不同交互动态与本体的真实世界训练与评估套件，评估套件包含长时多段片段及成功与失败执行。

Result: 在七种代表性ACWMs上的实验揭示了各模型不同的优势与失效模式。

Conclusion: WorldOracle为衡量和推进面向操作任务的动作条件世界建模提供了系统性基础，并将发布数据集、评估代码和排行榜。

Abstract: We introduce WorldOracle, a benchmark specifically designed to evaluate action-conditioned world models (ACWMs) against paired real-world executions. Given the same initial observation and low-level action sequence, WorldOracle asks whether a predicted rollout reproduces what happens in the real world, including physical consequences and task-state transitions. We evaluate models along four complementary dimensions: physical state correctness, interaction spatial alignment, key interaction area fidelity, and multi-view action coherence. To support this evaluation, we curate real-world training and evaluation suites that cover different interaction dynamics and embodiments. The evaluation suite contains long, multi-segment episodes with both successful and failed executions, enabling evaluation across varied action outcomes and behavior distributions. Experiments on seven representative ACWMs reveal distinct model strengths and failure modes. WorldOracle provides a systematic foundation for measuring and advancing action-conditioned world modeling for manipulation. We will release the datasets, evaluation code, and leaderboard.

</details>


### [244] [RoboSimStudio: An Interactive Toolkit for Rapid Real2Sim of Robot Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18835)
*Kaiyuan Chen,Shuangyu Xie,Uksang Yoo,Xinyue Zhu,Cael Magner,Alvin Zhu,Daehwa Kim,Eric Heiden,Erwin Coumans,Miles Macklin,Spencer Huang,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboSimStudio（RSS），一个可交互工具包，将求解器、接触模型与物理参数的选择显式化并可编辑，支持人类与编码智能体基于观测到的物理行为迭代精化机器人操作仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人与其所操作物体之间相互作用的物理建模十分困难，涉及接触力学、耦合接触动力学、形变、刚度与摩擦等因素，亟需一种能显式控制和调整仿真设置的手段。

Method: 基于开源 Newton 物理仿真系统实现 RSS，使求解器、接触模型和物理参数显式可编辑，并开展人类引导的 real2sim 实验：视觉评估、力反馈以及智能体化的 Real2Sim2Real。

Result: 通过三类实验（视觉评估、力反馈、智能体化 Real2Sim2Real）进行了人类引导的 real2sim 验证，展示了该工具包在迭代精化仿真方面的效果。

Conclusion: RSS 通过显式、可编辑的仿真设置与迭代精化流程，为机器人物体操作的仿真建模提供实用工具；代码、视频及更多信息见项目网站。

Abstract: It is difficult to accurately model the physics of the interaction between a robot and the objects it manipulates, including contact mechanics, coupled contact dynamics, deformations, stiffness and friction. We present RoboSimStudio (RSS), an interactive toolkit that makes choices of solver, contact model, and physical parameters explicit and editable, allowing both humans and coding agents to iteratively refine simulations based on observed physical behavior. We implement RSS with the open Newton physics simulation system and describe experiments with human-guided real2sim using (1) visual evaluation, (2) force-feedback and (3) agentic Real2Sim2Real. Code, video and additional information can be found on the project website.

</details>


### [245] [LIBERO-RIG: A Hardware-Setup Robustness Benchmark for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18843)
*Andrea Yaoyun Cui,Hanyu Wang,Xinyi Wang,Bangzheng Li,Lin Zhang,Zhenggang Tang,Shyamal Buch,Dejia Xu,Matthew Tancik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LIBERO-RIG 基准，在保持 LIBERO 任务不变的前提下仅扰动机器人硬件与配置（十一类扰动轴、每轴五级严重度），评估九个 VLA 模型后发现成功率随严重度大幅下降，且视觉扰动训练无法缓解视觉不变类故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略需在部署环境中泛化，但现实中机器人自身会发生变化：相机被碰动或重装、标定漂移、控制器频率不同、末端垫磨损等。现有基准多关注任务或场景变化，缺乏对机器人硬件与配置扰动的系统评测。

Method: 构建 LIBERO-RIG 基准，固定所有 LIBERO 任务，仅扰动机器人硬件与设置。涵盖十一类扰动轴：相机安装与光学、相机-机械臂标定、驱动增益、控制频率、本体感知噪声、基座位姿、桌面高度、夹爪垫等；每轴五级严重度，在四个 LIBERO 套件上共生成 210 种条件。对九个近期视觉-语言-动作模型，每条件评测 500 回合，并用严格统计检验验证结论。

Result: 汇总成功率在中等严重度从 0.932 降至 0.767，最高严重度降至 0.509。性能退化程度在不同扰动轴与动作接口间差异显著；针对视觉扰动的训练无法缓解那些不改变相机图像的故障。

Conclusion: LIBERO-RIG 揭示当前 VLA 模型对机器人自身硬件与配置扰动的脆弱性，且视觉层面训练不足以覆盖非视觉故障。作者发布该基准及每回合固定扰动参数的清单，以推动机器人策略的硬件级泛化研究。

Abstract: Robot policies are expected to generalize across deployment environments, including variations in the robot itself: cameras are bumped and remounted, calibrations drift, controllers run at different rates, and end-effector pads wear down. We present LIBERO-RIG, a benchmark that perturbs only the robot's hardware and setup while holding every LIBERO task fixed. The benchmark spans eleven perturbation axes, including camera mounting and optics, camera-to-arm calibration, actuation gain, control rate, proprioceptive noise, base pose, table height, and gripper pads. Each axis has five severity levels, yielding 210 conditions across the four LIBERO suites. We evaluate nine recent vision-language-action models using 500 episodes per condition and substantiate each finding with rigorous statistical tests. Pooled success falls from 0.932 to 0.767 at the middle severity and 0.509 at the highest severity. Performance degradation varies substantially across perturbation axes and action interfaces, and training on visual perturbations does not mitigate faults that leave camera images unchanged. We release the benchmark together with manifests specifying the fixed perturbation parameters for each episode.

</details>


### [246] [Contact Trajectory Prompting: In-Context Transfer of Contact-Rich Behaviors from a Single Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18848)
*Xian Nie,Yujie Zang,Yuhang Zheng,Yupeng Zheng,Songen Gu,Wendi Chen,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu,Wenchao Ding,Junchi Yan,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出接触轨迹提示（CTP）框架，通过单次演示实现接触丰富操作行为的一次性迁移，在真实擦拭、旋转和插入任务上达到78.7%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文机器人学习可从演示中获取新行为而无需测试时参数更新，但仅靠视觉和运动学规格难以区分动作相似却需要不同交互强度或模式的接触丰富行为。

Method: CTP从单次演示中编码运动与接触信息，构建有序的接触轨迹提示，捕捉其时间演化并指定期望的物理交互行为；执行时策略利用在线感知反馈从提示中检索交互相关信息，以演示的接触行为为条件进行闭环动作生成。

Result: 在训练集未包含的真实擦拭、旋转和插入任务上，CTP总体成功率达78.7%，超出最强基线13.0个百分点；仅更改参考演示即可遵循强度与方向的接触规格，包括中间擦拭力水平和顺/逆时针瓶盖旋转。

Conclusion: 接触轨迹可作为有效的行为规格，将上下文机器人学习从动作模仿扩展到物理交互迁移。

Abstract: In-context robot learning enables robots to acquire new behaviors from demonstrations without test-time parameter updates. However, visual and kinematic specifications alone may not distinguish contact-rich behaviors that share similar motions but require different interaction intensities or patterns. We propose Contact Trajectory Prompting (CTP), a framework for one-shot transfer of contact-rich manipulation behaviors. CTP encodes motion and contact information from a single demonstration into an ordered contact trajectory prompt that captures their temporal evolution and specifies the desired physical interaction behavior. During execution, the policy uses online sensory feedback to retrieve interaction-relevant information from the prompt, conditioning closed-loop action generation on the demonstrated contact behavior. Across real-world wiping, rotation, and insertion tasks on objects excluded from training, CTP achieves 78.7% overall success, outperforming the strongest evaluated baseline by 13.0 percentage points.
